"""Candidate116 reward API. Legacy equations are retained for diagnostics/off-request.
Every training term is explicitly present in selected_optimization_terms.
"""
import torch
from legacy_fudan111_reward_diagnostics_v1 import (
    BoundOwnStepReward,UPSTREAM_COMMIT,NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE_OR_STATEFUL_ORIGINAL_TERMS,
    _weighted,instantaneous_jump_terms,instantaneous_stair_recovery_terms,JUMP_WEIGHTS,RECOVERY_WEIGHTS,
    contact_and_step_modes as legacy_modes)
from direct_wheel_height_command_reward_v1 import CommandReward,modes as command_modes
from jump_process_command_reward122_v1 import evaluate
PARAMETERS=CommandReward()

def contact_and_step_modes(low,high,force_z,root_x,root_vz,p):
    assert p.step_top_z_m==PARAMETERS.step_height and p.step_edge_x_m==PARAMETERS.edge_x and p.platform_end_x_m==PARAMETERS.far_x
    old=legacy_modes(low,high,force_z,root_x,root_vz,p)
    new=command_modes(low,high,force_z,root_x,PARAMETERS)
    old.update(new,top_support=new['top_support'],jump=new['near']*(1-new['top_support']),recovery=1-new['near']*(1-new['top_support']),
        command_forward_velocity=new['base_command_vx']*(1-new['top_support']),wheel_bottom2=low[...,2],
        command_reward116=True,legacy_flight_is_diagnostic_only=True)
    return old

def combined_reward(m,p,mode):
    j,jparts=_weighted(instantaneous_jump_terms(m,p),JUMP_WEIGHTS,2.5,p.control_dt_s)
    r,rparts=_weighted(instantaneous_stair_recovery_terms(m,p),RECOVERY_WEIGHTS,1.,p.control_dt_s)
    # Preserve original penalty magnitudes only as a small bounded attenuation;
    # they cannot turn all task rewards negative and incentivize early falling.
    effort_names=('action_rate','torques','dof_pos_limits','nominal_state','orientation','ang_vel_xy','dof_vel')
    penalty=sum(rparts[k] for k in effort_names)/p.control_dt_s
    assert bool((penalty<=1e-6).all())
    effort=torch.exp(.05*penalty.clamp_min(-10.))
    data=dict(wheel_bottom2=mode['wheel_bottom2'],world_velocity3=torch.cat((m['task_planar_velocity'],m['world_vertical_velocity'][...,None]),-1),
        world_angular_velocity3=m['world_angular_velocity'],request_active=m['request_active'],heading_cos=m['heading_cos'],upright_cos=m['upright_cos'],root_y=m['root_lateral_position'])
    data.update(wheel_high2=m['wheel_high2'],wheel_bottom_velocity2=m['wheel_bottom_velocity2'])
    out=evaluate(data,mode,r,effort,crossed=m['process_crossed'],closure_m=m['closure_m'],public_age=m['public_age'],p=PARAMETERS)
    assert bool(((out['total']>=-.02-1e-6)|(m['request_active']==0)).all())
    return dict(total=out['total'],selected_optimization_terms=out['selected_terms'],
        process_command122=out,command_tracking116=dict(command_vx=out['command_vx'],target_each_wheel_bottom_m=PARAMETERS.step_height,
            actual_wheel_bottom2=mode['wheel_bottom2'],both_wheel_height_score=out['both_wheel_height_score'],
            forward_command_fraction_squared=(m['task_planar_velocity'][...,0]/.5).clamp(0,1).square(),near_both_wheels=mode['near'],effort_quality=effort,
            original_leg_length_flight_height_bonuses_not_selected=True,original_flight_is_not_airborne_truth=True),
        jump_reward=j,recovery_reward=r,jump_terms=jparts,recovery_terms=rparts,
        legacy_jump_reward_is_diagnostic_not_optimization=True,actual_success_not_inferred=True,
        own_top_support_quiet_placement=mode['top_support']*m['process_crossed']*torch.exp(-m['world_vertical_velocity'].square()/.15**2),top_quiet=out['selected_terms']['quiet_top_after_qualified_cross'],
        contact_step_modes=mode,unsupported_original_terms=NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE_OR_STATEFUL_ORIGINAL_TERMS)

def H(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def put(path,value):
    path=Path(path);tmp=path.with_suffix(path.suffix+'.partial')
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(value,indent=2,allow_nan=False),'utf8');tmp.replace(path)

"""Native127 MOD4 step adapter. Source candidate; requires new typed admission.

Fudan-inspired state-conditioned reward, static terminal task-space goal.
It is neither the upstream two-policy switching controller nor a measured
joint-motion reference. Original real270mm success remains independently exact.
"""
import math
from jump_process_frame122_v2 import advance,with_code,inject_goal_cues,unpack as process_unpack
from jump_process_geometry122_v1 import OwnProcessGeometry,wheel_bottom_velocity2

class OwnFudanStepReference:
    def __init__(self,document,device='cpu',*,order_scope='unbound'):
        require(document['schema']=='mod4.own_fudan_step_terminal_goal.v1','typed Fudan step goal')
        self.document=document;self.device=torch.device(device);self.order_scope=order_scope
        self.names=tuple(document['joint_names']);self.metadata=document['metadata']
        require(len(self.names)==len(set(self.names))==30,'named physical30')
        require(self.metadata['control_dt_s']==.01 and self.metadata['request_age_ticks']==200 and self.metadata['request_end_exclusive']==2000 and self.metadata['episode_seconds']==20.,'native100Hz coherent20s request')
        require(self.metadata['physical270_success_unchanged'] and not self.metadata['source_is_measured_successful_Fudan_joint_motion'],'own task goal, no counterfeit teacher')
        self.neutral=torch.tensor(document['neutral_packed73'],dtype=torch.float32,device=self.device)
        self.joint_scale=torch.tensor(self.metadata['joint_position_range_scale30'],dtype=torch.float32,device=self.device)
        require(self.neutral.shape==(73,) and self.joint_scale.shape==(30,) and torch.isfinite(self.neutral).all() and (self.joint_scale>0).all(),'finite source shapes')
        self.leg_ids=[self.names.index(x) for x in self.metadata['actuator_leg_joint_names']]
        self.parameters=BoundOwnStepReward(**self.metadata['bound_own_step_reward'])
    def bind_named_order(self,names,*,scope):
        names=tuple(names);require(scope in ('source_CPU_fixture','actual_SDK_readback') and len(names)==30 and set(names)==set(self.names),'exact named order')
        perm=[self.names.index(x) for x in names];cols=[*range(13),*[13+i for i in perm],*[43+i for i in perm]]
        doc=dict(self.document,joint_names=list(names),neutral_packed73=self.neutral[cols].cpu().tolist());doc['metadata']=dict(self.metadata,joint_position_range_scale30=self.joint_scale[perm].cpu().tolist())
        return OwnFudanStepReference(doc,self.device,order_scope=scope)
    def to(self,device):return OwnFudanStepReference(self.document,device,order_scope=self.order_scope)
    def active(self,frames):return decode_private_frame(frames)['public_age']>=200
    def query(self,frames):
        active=self.active(frames);p=self.parameters;value=self.neutral.expand(*frames.shape,73).clone()
        value[...,:2]=0;value[...,0]=torch.where(active,value.new_tensor(p.goal_root_x_m),value.new_tensor(0.))
        value[...,2]=p.standing_body_height_m+active.to(value.dtype)*p.step_top_z_m
        value[...,3:7]=value.new_tensor([1.,0.,0.,0.]);value[...,7:13]=0;value[...,43:73]=0
        mask=torch.zeros_like(value);mask[...,:13]=1
        return value,mask
    def future_goal(self,frames,num_frames):
        base=torch.cat([torch.cat(self._masked_query(frames+i),-1) for i in range(num_frames)],-1)
        require(num_frames==10,"typed process goals keep10 frames")
        return inject_goal_cues(base,frames)
    def _masked_query(self,frames):
        value,mask=self.query(frames);return value*mask,mask

class OwnFudanStepMdp(ConvertedMOD4JumpMdp):
    max_step_reward=.16  # conservative clipped-component upper bound, log normalization only
    def __init__(self,provider,contract,geometry,leg_encoder_joint_names=None):
        super().__init__(provider,leg_encoder_joint_names);self.import_contract=contract;self.authored_geometry=geometry
        self.wheel_geometry=OwnStepWheelGeometry(contract,geometry,provider.names).to(provider.device)
        self.process_geometry=OwnProcessGeometry(geometry,list(self.wheel_geometry.body_names)).to(provider.device)
        closures=list(contract['closures'].values())
        self.closure_i0=torch.tensor([self.wheel_geometry.body_names.index(x['body0']) for x in closures],device=provider.device)
        self.closure_i1=torch.tensor([self.wheel_geometry.body_names.index(x['body1']) for x in closures],device=provider.device)
        self.closure_a0=torch.tensor([x['anchor0_m'] for x in closures],device=provider.device,dtype=torch.float32)
        self.closure_a1=torch.tensor([x['anchor1_m'] for x in closures],device=provider.device,dtype=torch.float32)
        self.reward_parameters=provider.parameters;p=provider.parameters
        require(p.step_top_z_m==self.platform_top_z_m==.27 and p.step_edge_x_m==self.step_edge_x_m,'actual existing270 scene')
        self.leg_names=tuple(provider.metadata['actuator_leg_joint_names']);self.leg_indices=[provider.names.index(n) for n in self.leg_names]
        self.actuated_names=tuple(contract['actuated_joint_names']);require(set(self.leg_names).issubset(self.actuated_names) and len(self.actuated_names)==6,'actual six motors')
        self.actuated_indices=[provider.names.index(n) for n in self.actuated_names]
        self.hip_anchors=torch.tensor([contract['joints'][n]['local_pos0'] for n in ('left_hip','right_hip')],dtype=torch.float32,device=provider.device)
        require(all(contract['joints'][n]['parent']=='chassis' for n in ('left_hip','right_hip')),'recorded own hip anchors')
        limits=torch.tensor([contract['joints'][n]['limits_si'] for n in self.leg_names],dtype=torch.float32,device=provider.device)
        middle=limits.mean(-1);half=(limits[:,1]-limits[:,0])*.5*.97;self.soft_lower=middle-half;self.soft_upper=middle+half
    def to(self,device):return OwnFudanStepMdp(self.provider.to(device),self.import_contract,self.authored_geometry,self.leg_encoder_joint_names)
    # yaw_mat(R).T @ R has an identically zero (1,0) entry, barring
    # FP32 roundoff. In the existing 6D column encoding this is feature3
    # after the height. Replace only that redundant zero with .01*yaw/pi.
    # .01 is the inherited RunningNorm eps, giving a bounded <=1 signal
    # even when the old zero slot's variance/count is frozen. Goal/tilt,
    # all weights, all norm tensors and all three optimizers stay inherited.
    # Wrapped yaw has a declared representation seam at +/-pi; it is not
    # an independent RNG stream, a model reset, or a sine-only heading alias.
    @staticmethod
    def _with_task_heading(value,state):
        R=state[1]
        yaw=torch.atan2(R[...,1,0],R[...,0,0])
        return torch.cat((value[...,:3],(.01*yaw/math.pi).unsqueeze(-1),value[...,4:]),-1)
    def obs(self,state,previous_action,context,frames):
        return self._with_task_heading(super().obs(state,previous_action,context,frames),state)
    def critic_features(self,state,frames,normalized_context):
        return self._with_task_heading(super().critic_features(state,frames,normalized_context),state)
    def relative_features(self,state,frames):
        target,_=self.provider.query(frames);age=decode_private_frame(frames)['public_age'];shape=frames.shape;flat=age.reshape(-1)
        features=task_relative_observation(state[0].reshape(-1,3),target[...,:3].reshape(-1,3),state[0].new_full((flat.numel(),),self.step_edge_x_m),state[0].new_full((flat.numel(),),self.platform_top_z_m),flat).reshape(*shape,7)
        request=((age>=200)&(age<2000)).to(features.dtype)
        return torch.cat((features[...,:5],request.unsqueeze(-1),features[...,6:]),-1)
    def closure_error(self,g):
        p=g['body_position'];R=g['body_rotation']
        a=p[...,self.closure_i0,:]+(R[...,self.closure_i0,:,:]@self.closure_a0[...,None]).squeeze(-1)
        b=p[...,self.closure_i1,:]+(R[...,self.closure_i1,:,:]@self.closure_a1[...,None]).squeeze(-1)
        return (a-b).norm(dim=-1).amax(-1)
    def advance_reward_frame(self,state,next_state,frames):
        old=self.wheel_geometry(state);g=self.wheel_geometry(next_state)
        history=next_state[6][...,:18].reshape(*frames.shape,3,2,3)
        free=(history[...,:2,:,:].norm(dim=-1).amax(-2)<=1).all(-1)
        clean=(g['low'][...,2]>.005).all(-1)&free&(self.process_geometry.nonwheel_clearance(g)>0)
        return advance(frames,clean_air=clean,root_vz=next_state[2][...,2],previous_front2=old['high'][...,0],next_front2=g['high'][...,0],wheel_bottom2=g['low'][...,2])
    def frame_with_real_process(self,frames,code):return with_code(frames,code)
    def objective_terms(self,state,frames,action,previous_action):
        p,R,v,w,q,dq,aux=state;g=self.wheel_geometry(state);Rt=R.transpose(-1,-2)
        body_v=(Rt@v.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1);body_w=(Rt@w.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)
        local_wheels=(Rt.unsqueeze(-3)@(g['centre']-p.unsqueeze(-2)).unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)-self.hip_anchors
        L0=local_wheels[...,[0,2]].norm(dim=-1);theta=torch.atan2(local_wheels[...,0],-local_wheels[...,2])
        history=aux[...,:18].reshape(*frames.shape,3,2,3);fz=torch.maximum(history[...,0,:,2],history[...,1,:,2])
        modes=contact_and_step_modes(g['low'],g['high'],fz,p[...,0],v[...,2],self.reward_parameters)
        active=self.provider.active(frames).to(p.dtype);modes['jump']=modes['jump']*active;modes['recovery']=1-modes['jump'];modes['command_forward_velocity']=modes['command_forward_velocity']*active
        leg_q=q[...,self.leg_indices];violation=(self.soft_lower-leg_q).clamp_min(0).sum(-1)+(leg_q-self.soft_upper).clamp_min(0).sum(-1)
        projected_gravity=-R[...,2,:]
        measurements=dict(flight=modes['flight'],takeoff=modes['takeoff'],virtual_leg_length=L0,virtual_leg_angle=theta,
            world_vertical_velocity=v[...,2],body_vertical_velocity=body_v[...,2],body_forward_velocity=body_v[...,0],body_yaw_velocity=body_w[...,2],
            task_planar_velocity=v[...,:2],task_command_planar_velocity=torch.stack((modes['command_forward_velocity'],torch.zeros_like(v[...,2])),-1),
            command_forward_velocity=modes['command_forward_velocity'],command_yaw_velocity=torch.zeros_like(v[...,2]),root_height=p[...,2],
            relative_base_height=p[...,2],top_support=modes['top_support'],
            upright_cos=R[...,2,2],heading_cos=R[...,0,0]/R[...,:2,0].norm(dim=-1).clamp_min(1e-6),
            root_lateral_position=p[...,1],request_active=active,
            action=action,previous_action=previous_action,
            actuated_torque=aux[...,18:48][...,self.actuated_indices],actuated_leg_dq=dq[...,self.leg_indices],
            projected_gravity=projected_gravity,body_angular_velocity=body_w,world_angular_velocity=w,actuated_soft_limit_violation=violation,root_planar_velocity=v[...,:2])
        measurements.update(wheel_high2=g['high'],wheel_bottom_velocity2=wheel_bottom_velocity2(g,self.wheel_geometry),process_crossed=process_unpack(frames)['crossed'],closure_m=self.closure_error(g),public_age=decode_private_frame(frames)['public_age'])
        result=combined_reward(measurements,self.reward_parameters,modes);result['contact_step_modes']=modes;return result
    def reward(self,state,frames,action,previous_action):
        result=self.objective_terms(state,frames,action,previous_action)
        if torch.is_inference_mode_enabled():self.last_reward_components=result
        return result['total']
    def soft_termination(self,state,frames):
        # Physical low_base is disabled at -1m; do not invent a .12m hard failure.
        # Declare the sigmoid as a 32-control horizon risk (engineering surrogate),
        # not the same full risk repeated every 10ms. Exact physical tilt/timeout
        # masks remain certain here; all real physics termination functions stay unchanged.
        cos_tilt=state[1][...,2,2]
        horizon_risk=torch.sigmoid((math.cos(.75)-cos_tilt)/.04)
        soft=-torch.expm1(torch.log1p(-horizon_risk.clamp_max(1-1e-6))/32.)
        certain=(cos_tilt<math.cos(.75))|(decode_private_frame(frames)['public_age']>=2000)
        original=torch.where(certain,torch.ones_like(soft),soft)
        return combine_closure_risk(original,self.closure_error(self.wheel_geometry(state)),decode_private_frame(frames)['public_age'])
    def valid_imagination_start(self,frames,horizon):
        row=decode_private_frame(frames);return (frames>=0)&(row['delay_ticks']<250)&(row['public_age']<2000)
    def contract_manifest(self):
        return dict(schema='mod4.native_supermimic.fudan_inspired_own270_step.v1',upstream_commit=UPSTREAM_COMMIT,
            packed_state=127,dynamic_aux=54,static_xi=31,actor_observation=86,goal_frame=146,future_goal_frames_expected=10,action_dim=6,
            reference_metadata=self.provider.metadata,reference_order_scope=self.provider.order_scope,
            virtual_leg_definition='own hip anchor to exact wheel-mesh centre projected to chassis sagittal XZ',
            physical_timeout_seconds=20.,selected_reset_RNG_action_PD_delay_DR_and_success_unchanged=True,
            contact_modes_refreshed_on_every_actual_or_predicted_state=True,root_goal_is_terminal_task_pose_not_limb_mocap=True,
            actual270_cross_fullfootprint_topcontact12_stable200_callbacks_required=True,
            reward_velocity_frame='fixed_platform_world_XY',actor_orientation_frame='original_yaw_invariant_tilt_plus_bounded_explicit_world_yaw_in_redundant_zero_slot',
            goal_and_goal_normalizer_unchanged=True,own_top_quiet_placement_weight=12.,
            task_alignment_bonus_max_per_control=.01,platform_full_XY_bounds=True,
            height_rewards_two_actual_planes_not_interpolated=True,soft_tilt_risk_controls=32,
            physical_low_base_disabled_minus1=True,quiet_includes_world_vertical_velocity_heading_and_tilt=True,
            not_unmodified_upstream_reward_or_two_policy_controller=True,source_candidate_not_installed=True,successful_skill=False,reward_process122=dict(frame_low20_original_delay4096_age=True,process_memory_high_bits=True,goal_cue_slots=list(range(43,49)),cue_slots_are_dimensionless_not_joint_velocity_targets=True,original_network_parameter_and_norm_shapes_unchanged=True,physical_air_witness_original16callbacks=True,planning_air_is_authored_geometry_and_wheel_history_surrogate=True,landing_reward_requires_qualified_launch_and_both_edge_crosses=True,closure_penalty_and_horizon_risk_are_engineering_not_calibrated_failure_probability=True,physical2mm_guard_unchanged=True))

"""New task request clock and command, preserving the exact original270 event truth."""
import types
import torch
from native_wheel_leg_bridge_v4 import NativeWheelLegBridge
from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.contract import SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY
from private_phase_frame_v1 import encode
from jump_process_runtime122_v1 import current_process_code

class FudanStepBridge(NativeWheelLegBridge):
    def __init__(self,*args,**kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args,**kwargs)
        self.own_env_ids=torch.arange(self.num_envs,device=self.device,dtype=torch.long)
        self._real_reward_tracking=None
    def _encode_current_frame(self,state):
        row=state.info[SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY]
        row['f']=encode(row['f'],self.own_env_ids,current_process_code(self.real))
        return state
    def init_state(self):return self._encode_current_frame(super().init_state())
    def step(self,actions):
        result=super().step(actions)
        terms=self.mdp.last_reward_components
        # These tensors come from the actual terminal pre-reset state already
        # used for the native reward, never a second actor or world-model call.
        def means(value):
            return {k:means(v) if isinstance(v,dict) else v.detach().mean() for k,v in value.items() if isinstance(v,(dict,torch.Tensor))}
        self._real_reward_tracking=means(terms)
        return self._encode_current_frame(result)
    def reward_tracking_snapshot(self):
        def plain(value):return {k:plain(v) if isinstance(v,dict) else float(v.cpu()) for k,v in value.items()}
        return dict(scope='last_complete_actual_control_mean_over_all4096_not_frozen_skill_evaluation',components=None if self._real_reward_tracking is None else plain(self._real_reward_tracking),step_top_z_m=.27,request_control_interval=[200,2000],physical_success_from_original270_counter_only=True)

def install_fudan_step_request_and_command(env):
    import spin_commands
    from h1_original_directional_runtime import audit as directional_audit
    from h1_external_operator_command_v1 import H1ExternalOperatorCommand
    assert not getattr(env,'_own_fudan_step_task_installed',False)
    phase=env.h1_phase
    def request(this):return (this.age_control>=200)&(this.age_control<2000)
    phase.public_request=types.MethodType(request,phase)
    def publish(this):
        this._mod4_mode_bit=this.h1_phase.public_mode()
        term=this.command_manager.get_term('twist');term.set_manual_evaluation_mode();term.vel_command_b.zero_()
        term.vel_command_b[:,0]=this.h1_phase.public_request().to(term.vel_command_b.dtype)*.5*(~this.h1_phase.goal.success).to(term.vel_command_b.dtype)
    def audit(this):
        result=directional_audit(this);term=this.command_manager.get_term('twist')
        assert isinstance(term,H1ExternalOperatorCommand)
        expected=torch.zeros_like(term.command);expected[:,0]=phase.public_request().to(expected.dtype)*.5*(~phase.goal.success).to(expected.dtype)
        assert torch.equal(term.command,expected)
        result.update(stage='MOD4_Fudan106_real270_20s_task',request_control_interval=[200,2000],approach_command_m_s=.5,zero_yaw_and_lateral=True,original_physical_reset_RNG_unchanged=True,original_true270_cross_footprint_topcontact12_stable200_unchanged=True,public_schedule_explicitly_changed_from100_180=True)
        return result
    env._h1_publish_external_request=types.MethodType(publish,env);spin_commands.audit=audit
    env._own_fudan_step_task_installed=True;env._h1_publish_external_request()
    return audit(env)

"""Source-only MOD4 native bridge for explicit dynamic54 / static31.

Original vec.step action object and output are preserved; terminal state comes
from the original trace's pre-reset snapshot. This is not a deployed worker.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
import torch
from actual_DR_memory_getter_v1 import ActualDRMemoryGetter, read_old_processed_applied, tensor
from static_dynamic_delay_contract_v1 import pack_dynamic_aux
from compact_episode_static31_v1 import compact_static_snapshot
from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.geometry import unflatten_state
from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.contract import SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY, SUPERMIMIC_TRANSITION_KEY

@dataclass
class NativeBridgeState:
    info: dict
    reward: torch.Tensor
    terminated: torch.Tensor
    truncated: torch.Tensor
    obs: dict

class CompactStatic31Cache:
    def __init__(self, env, binding):
        self.env=env
        self.reader=ActualDRMemoryGetter(env,binding)
        self.cache=None
    def _project(self):
        e=self.env;pd=e._explicit_pd;dr=e._mild_dr
        self.cache=compact_static_snapshot(self.reader.snapshot(),
            nominal_mass=dr.mass,nominal_inertia=dr.inertia,
            original_body_com=e._chassis_com.original_pose[...,:3],
            body_id=e._chassis_com.body_id,
            leg_ids=pd.leg_ids,wheel_ids=pd.wheel_ids,
            gas_ids=e._spring_pressure.ids,wheel_shape_ids=dr.wheel_shapes,
            nominal_gas_stiffness=e._spring_pressure.nominal)
    def capture_initial(self):
        self.reader.capture_initial();self._project()
        return self.xi()
    def refresh_after_resets(self,ids):
        previous=self.cache
        row=self.reader.refresh_after_resets(ids)
        if row['updated']:
            self._project()
            selected=torch.zeros(self.env.num_envs,device=self.env.device,dtype=torch.bool)
            selected[ids]=True
            if not torch.equal(previous[~selected],self.cache[~selected]):
                raise ValueError('nonreset static31 changed')
        return row
    def xi(self):
        if self.cache is None:raise ValueError('static31 initial capture missing')
        return self.cache.clone()

class NativeWheelLegBridge:
    def __init__(self, runner, trace, mdp, runtime_order_binding, before_action_hook):
        if not callable(before_action_hook):
            raise ValueError('original fullsave/trace/deadline before-action hook required')
        self.runner=runner;self.vec=runner.env;self.real=self.vec.unwrapped
        self.trace=trace;self.mdp=mdp;self.pre_action_hook=before_action_hook
        self.num_envs=self.real.num_envs;self.device=torch.device(self.real.device)
        if self.real.physics_dt!=.0025 or self.real.step_dt!=.01 or self.real.cfg.decimation!=4:
            raise ValueError('original 2.5ms/100Hz/decimation4 required')
        if mdp.num_joints!=30 or mdp.auxiliary_dim!=54 or mdp.packed_state_dim!=127 or mdp.context_dim!=31 or mdp.action_dim!=6:
            raise ValueError('explicit physical30/dynamic54/static31/action6 MDP required')
        self.static=CompactStatic31Cache(self.real,runtime_order_binding)
        self.static.capture_initial()
        self.ep_step=torch.zeros(self.num_envs,device=self.device,dtype=torch.int32)
        self.state=None
        self._pre_reset_hook_installed=False
        self._terminal_aux54=None

    @property
    def supermimic_mdp(self):return self.mdp

    def install_exact_pre_reset_aux_hook(self):
        if self._pre_reset_hook_installed:
            raise ValueError('duplicate native pre-reset auxiliary hook')
        old=self.real.termination_manager.compute
        bridge=self
        def compute(*args,**kwargs):
            result=old(*args,**kwargs)
            frame=bridge.trace.frame
            if frame is None:
                raise ValueError('original complete pre-reset trace frame missing')
            # Reuse the original copies of contact/torque; only queue memory is new.
            applied=read_old_processed_applied(bridge.real,int(bridge.real._sim_step_counter))
            bridge._terminal_aux54=pack_dynamic_aux(
                frame['history5ms'],frame['applied_torque'],applied)
            from jump_process_runtime122_v1 import current_process_code
            bridge._terminal_process_code=current_process_code(bridge.real).clone()
            return result
        self.real.termination_manager.compute=compute
        self._pre_reset_hook_installed=True

    @torch.inference_mode()
    def pack(self,root,joint,velocity,aux):
        root=root.detach().clone()
        root[:,:3]-=self.real.scene.env_origins
        # Actual SDK root_state_w xyzw -> native wxyz, already source-verified.
        root[:,3:7]=root[:,[6,3,4,5]]
        value=torch.cat((root,joint.detach(),velocity.detach(),aux.detach()),-1)
        if value.shape!=(self.num_envs,127) or not torch.isfinite(value).all():
            raise ValueError('invalid physical73+dynamic54 packed state')
        return value

    @torch.inference_mode()
    def current(self):
        e=self.real;r=e.scene['robot']
        root=tensor(r.data.root_state_w)
        joint=tensor(r.data.joint_pos)
        velocity=tensor(r.data.joint_vel)
        applied_torque=tensor(r.data.applied_torque)
        aux=pack_dynamic_aux(e._integrated_contact.history,applied_torque,
                            read_old_processed_applied(e,int(e._sim_step_counter)))
        return self.pack(root,joint,velocity,aux),self.static.xi()

    def init_state(self):
        if not self._pre_reset_hook_installed:
            raise ValueError('mandatory pre-reset state capture hook missing')
        s,xi=self.current();zero=torch.zeros(self.num_envs,device=self.device)
        self.state=NativeBridgeState(info={SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY:dict(
            s=s,xi=xi,f=self.real.h1_phase.age_control.clone(),
            a_prev=torch.zeros(self.num_envs,6,device=self.device),ep_step=self.ep_step.clone())},
            reward=zero,terminated=zero.bool(),truncated=zero.bool(),obs={})
        return self.state

    def before_action(self):
        return self.pre_action_hook()

    @torch.inference_mode()
    def step(self,actions):
        if self.state is None:raise ValueError('init_state required before native step')
        old=self.state.info[SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY]
        self._terminal_aux54=None
        try:
            # Exact original incoming object, values and original vec.step behavior.
            output=self.vec.step(actions)
            _,physical_reward,done,_=output
            frame=self.trace.frame
            if frame is None or self._terminal_aux54 is None:
                raise ValueError('mandatory terminal pre-reset auxiliary state missing')
            s1=self.pack(frame['root13'],frame['q30'],frame['joint_velocity'],self._terminal_aux54)
            done=done.bool()
            applied_ingress=frame['executed6'].detach().clone()
            # The native tracking head must agree on real and imagined state.
            # Original V8 RewardManager still runs and remains in the full trace.
            native_reward=self.mdp.reward(unflatten_state(s1),self.mdp.frame_with_real_process(old['f']+1,self._terminal_process_code),
                                           applied_ingress,old['a_prev']).detach()
            if native_reward.shape!=(self.num_envs,) or not torch.isfinite(native_reward).all():
                raise ValueError('invalid real native tracking reward')
            transition=dict(s=old['s'],s1=s1,a=applied_ingress,
                a_prev=old['a_prev'],xi=old['xi'],r=native_reward,f=old['f'],
                ep_step=old['ep_step'],done=done)
            if bool(done.any()):
                self.static.refresh_after_resets(torch.where(done)[0])
            s,xi=self.current()
            self.ep_step=torch.where(done,0,self.ep_step+1).to(torch.int32)
            previous=applied_ingress.clone();previous[done]=0
            self.state=NativeBridgeState(info={
                SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY:dict(s=s,xi=xi,f=self.real.h1_phase.age_control.clone(),
                                         a_prev=previous,ep_step=self.ep_step.clone()),
                SUPERMIMIC_TRANSITION_KEY:transition,
                'physical_V8_reward_snapshot':physical_reward.detach()},
                reward=native_reward,terminated=done,truncated=torch.zeros_like(done),obs={})
            return self.state
        finally:
            self._terminal_aux54=None

"""Differentiable recorded own30-joint tree FK and exact wheel mesh supports.

Only source JSON constants; no USD, SDK, model, physics or IK queries.

def qmat(q):
 q=q/q.norm(dim=-1,keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-12);w,x,y,z=q.unbind(-1)
 return torch.stack((1-2*(y*y+z*z),2*(x*y-w*z),2*(x*z+w*y),2*(x*y+w*z),1-2*(x*x+z*z),2*(y*z-w*x),2*(x*z-w*y),2*(y*z+w*x),1-2*(x*x+y*y)),-1).reshape(*q.shape[:-1],3,3)

    motion=torch.stack((one,zero,zero,zero,c,-s,zero,s,c),-1).reshape(*a.shape,3,3)
    CR=A@motion@R1T;cw=pw+axis*ad[...,None];offset=mv(CR,p1.expand_as(pp));cp=pp+anchor-offset;cv=pv+torch.cross(pw,anchor,dim=-1)-torch.cross(cw,offset,dim=-1)

"""Packed private phase lookup frame; original public age/reset/RNG unchanged.

Units:100Hz control ticks. delay=env_id%250, frame=delay*4096+public_age.
The signed phase_age=public_age-delay retains initial200tick standing semantics.
No learner/replay modification, sampling, SDK or device-to-host scalar reads.
"""
import torch
from jump_process_frame122_v2 import BASE,unpack as process_unpack
STRIDE=4096
DELAY_PERIOD_TICKS=250
PUBLIC_EPISODE_MAX_TICKS=2000
LOOKAHEAD_LIMITS={'world':32,'policy':10}

def _long(x):
 assert torch.is_tensor(x) and x.dtype==torch.long,'frame/age/env IDs must be long, no float truncation'
 return x

def encode(public_age,env_ids,process_code=None):
 """Return long packed frame; invalid actual ages/IDs get explicit sentinel-1."""
 age,ids=torch.broadcast_tensors(_long(public_age),_long(env_ids))
 assert age.device==ids.device
 valid=(age>=0)&(age<=PUBLIC_EPISODE_MAX_TICKS)&(ids>=0)
 frame=torch.remainder(ids,DELAY_PERIOD_TICKS)*STRIDE+age
 if process_code is not None:
  process_code=_long(process_code)
  valid=valid&(process_code>=0)&(process_code<256)&(((process_code>>1)&7)<=4)
  frame=frame+BASE*process_code
 return torch.where(valid,frame,torch.full_like(frame,-1))

def decode(frame):
 """Preserve actual public age and delayed phase age, even for future lookups.

 A predicted age2001 is not silently reset to0 or relabeled as actual age<=2000.
 valid_actual flags the original physical episode bounds without a GPU sync.
 """
 f=_long(frame);base=torch.remainder(f,BASE);delay=torch.div(base,STRIDE,rounding_mode='floor');age=torch.remainder(base,STRIDE)
 meta=torch.div(f,BASE,rounding_mode='floor')
 valid=(f>=0)&(meta<256)&(((meta>>1)&7)<=4)&(delay>=0)&(delay<DELAY_PERIOD_TICKS)&(age<=PUBLIC_EPISODE_MAX_TICKS)
 return dict(delay_ticks=delay,public_age=age,phase_age=age-delay,valid_actual=valid,process=process_unpack(f))

def horizon_checks(frame,lookahead,*,kind):
 """Per-row masks; caller retains original done/reset handling.

 within_episode means a horizon does not cross the actual20s reset boundary.
 lookup_encoding_valid also describes terminal lookups up to the admitted
32(world)/10(policy) offsets without falsely making those actual physical ages.
 """
 assert kind in LOOKAHEAD_LIMITS and type(lookahead)is int and 0<=lookahead<=LOOKAHEAD_LIMITS[kind]
 before=decode(frame);after=decode(frame+lookahead)
 same_delay=after['delay_ticks']==before['delay_ticks']
 lookup=before['valid_actual']&same_delay&(after['public_age']<=PUBLIC_EPISODE_MAX_TICKS+lookahead)
 return dict(within_episode=before['valid_actual']&after['valid_actual']&same_delay,
  lookup_encoding_valid=lookup,same_private_delay=same_delay,
  public_age=before['public_age'],phase_age=before['phase_age'],
  next_public_age=after['public_age'],next_phase_age=after['phase_age'])

def horizon_valid(frame,lookahead,*,kind):
 return horizon_checks(frame,lookahead,kind=kind)['within_episode']

"""Source-only wheel-leg state extension for the pinned SuperMimic BPTT loop.

The first six State components and their SE(3) integration match the frozen
vendor implementation. The seventh is dynamic auxiliary state, never static xi.
This module does not load checkpoints, start simulation or admit a Task.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
import torch
from torch import nn
from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.geometry import (
    invariant_features, mat_to_quat, quat_to_mat, yaw_mat, so3_exp,
)
from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.networks import (
    WorldModel as OriginalWorldModel, RunningNorm, mlp, run_mlp,
)

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WheelLegStateLayout:
    num_joints: int = 30
    auxiliary_dim: int = 54

    @property
    def packed_dim(self):
        return 13 + 2*self.num_joints + self.auxiliary_dim

    def unpack(self, value):
        if value.shape[-1] != self.packed_dim:
            raise ValueError(f"Expected explicit packed state width {self.packed_dim}, got {value.shape}")
        j = self.num_joints
        return (
            value[..., :3], quat_to_mat(value[..., 3:7]),
            value[..., 7:10], value[..., 10:13],
            value[..., 13:13+j], value[..., 13+j:13+2*j],
            value[..., 13+2*j:],
        )

    def pack(self, state):
        self.validate(state)
        p, R, v, w, q, dq, aux = state
        return torch.cat((p, mat_to_quat(R), v, w, q, dq, aux), -1)

    def validate(self, state):
        if len(state) != 7:
            raise ValueError("Wheel-leg state requires six physical components plus explicit auxiliary")
        batch = state[0].shape[:-1]
        expected = ((3,), (3,3), (3,), (3,), (self.num_joints,),
                    (self.num_joints,), (self.auxiliary_dim,))
        for i, (value, tail) in enumerate(zip(state, expected)):
            if value.shape != (*batch, *tail):
                raise ValueError(f"state component {i} has wrong shape {value.shape}")
            if value.dtype != state[0].dtype or value.device != state[0].device:
                raise ValueError("State component dtype/device mismatch")

    @staticmethod
    def stack(states, dim=0):
        return tuple(torch.stack([s[i] for s in states], dim) for i in range(7))


class AugmentedWheelLegWorldModel(nn.Module):
    """Predict original rigid-body increments and dynamic-aux increments together.

    Episode-fixed xi is a separate input. Terrain-relative coordinates prevent
    the old world-XY alias for a task with a fixed obstacle. No auxiliary variable
    is presented as a joint angle or silently kept constant through imagination.
    """
    def __init__(self, *, context_dim, hidden=(2048,2048,2048),
                 num_joints=30, auxiliary_dim=54, action_dim=6,
                 edge_x_m=.35, platform_top_z_m=.27):
        super().__init__()
        self.layout = WheelLegStateLayout(num_joints, auxiliary_dim)
        self.num_joints, self.context_dim = num_joints, context_dim
        self.action_dim = action_dim
        self.edge_x_m = float(edge_x_m)
        self.platform_top_z_m = float(platform_top_z_m)
        self.in_dim = 13+2*num_joints+auxiliary_dim+action_dim+context_dim+3
        self.out_dim = 12+2*num_joints+auxiliary_dim
        self.net = mlp(self.in_dim, hidden, self.out_dim,
                       act=nn.SiLU, layer_norm=True)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].bias)
        self.in_norm = RunningNorm(self.in_dim)
        self.out_norm = RunningNorm(self.out_dim, eps=1e-4)

    def terrain_relative(self, state):
        p = state[0]
        return torch.stack((self.edge_x_m-p[...,0], -p[...,1],
                            self.platform_top_z_m-p[...,2]), -1)

    def inputs(self, state, action, normalized_context):
        self.layout.validate(state)
        shape = state[0].shape[:-1]
        if action.shape != (*shape, self.action_dim):
            raise ValueError("Action width/batch differs from explicit six-actuator contract")
        if normalized_context.shape != (*shape, self.context_dim):
            raise ValueError("Static context width/batch differs from declared schema")
        features = invariant_features(state[:6]).clone()
        # Fixed-world terrain needs world heading as well as body-frame velocity.
        # Bounded continuous cues use two yaw-normalized orientation slots.
        # Input width, parameters, norms, integration and original targets stay unchanged.
        R = state[1]
        forward_x, forward_y = R[..., 0, 0], R[..., 1, 0]
        radius = torch.sqrt(forward_x.square()+forward_y.square()+1e-12)
        features[..., 1] += .005*(forward_x/radius-1.)
        features[..., 3] += .005*forward_y/radius
        return torch.cat((features, state[6],
                          action, normalized_context, self.terrain_relative(state)), -1)

    @staticmethod
    def targets(state, next_state):
        return torch.cat((OriginalWorldModel.targets(state[:6], next_state[:6]),
                          next_state[6]-state[6]), -1)

    @staticmethod
    def state_error(predicted_state, true_state):
        return torch.cat((OriginalWorldModel.state_error(predicted_state[:6], true_state[:6]),
                          predicted_state[6]-true_state[6]), -1)

    def step(self, state, action, normalized_context):
        p, R, v, w, q, dq, aux = state
        prediction = run_mlp(self.net, self.in_norm(self.inputs(state, action, normalized_context)))
        inc = prediction*self.out_norm.std()+self.out_norm.mean
        dp, dR, dv, dw, d_q, d_dq, d_aux = inc.split(
            (3,3,3,3,self.num_joints,self.num_joints,self.layout.auxiliary_dim), -1)
        heading = yaw_mat(R)
        return (
            p+(heading@dp.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1),
            R@so3_exp(dR),
            v+(R@dv.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1),
            w+(R@dw.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1),
            q+d_q, dq+d_dq, aux+d_aux,
        )


def require_architecture_source_migration(checkpoint_contract, current_contract):
    """Reject a legacy128/xi49 checkpoint being called an exact same-source resume."""
    if checkpoint_contract != current_contract:
        raise ValueError("Explicit architecture/source migration required; legacy parameters are not shape-compatible continuation")


def learner_error_floor(layout):
    # Same units/floors for original body and joint components. Aux normalized
    # labels use their own unit scale; a concrete runtime schema must pin it.
    return torch.cat((torch.full((3,),3e-3), torch.full((3,),5e-3),
                      torch.full((3,),2e-2), torch.full((3,),5e-2),
                      torch.full((layout.num_joints,),3e-3),
                      torch.full((layout.num_joints,),5e-2),
                      torch.full((layout.auxiliary_dim,),1e-3)))

"""Injected task contract for SuperMimic world-model training."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Protocol, runtime_checkable

import torch

State = tuple[
    torch.Tensor,
    torch.Tensor,
    torch.Tensor,
    torch.Tensor,
    torch.Tensor,
    torch.Tensor,
]

SUPERMIMIC_TRANSITION_KEY = "supermimic"
SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY = "supermimic_state"


@runtime_checkable
class SuperMimicMdpProtocol(Protocol):
    """Differentiable analytic heads and reference data owned by a task."""

    num_joints: int
    context_dim: int
    obs_dim: int
    goal_frame_dim: int
    max_step_reward: float

    def obs(
        self,
        state: State,
        previous_action: torch.Tensor,
        context: torch.Tensor,
        frames: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> torch.Tensor: ...

    def add_noise(self, obs: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor: ...

    def goal(self, frames: torch.Tensor, num_frames: int) -> torch.Tensor: ...

    def reward(
        self,
        state: State,
        frames: torch.Tensor,
        action: torch.Tensor,
        previous_action: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> torch.Tensor: ...

    def soft_termination(self, state: State, frames: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor: ...

    def critic_features(
        self,
        state: State,
        frames: torch.Tensor,
        normalized_context: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> torch.Tensor: ...

    def normalize_context(self, context: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor: ...

    def valid_imagination_start(self, frames: torch.Tensor, horizon: int) -> torch.Tensor: ...

    def to(self, device: str | torch.device) -> SuperMimicMdpProtocol:
        """Return a copy on ``device`` for learner-side use off the env device."""
        ...


@runtime_checkable
class SupportsSuperMimicProtocol(Protocol):
    """Environment exposing its task-owned SuperMimic implementation."""

    @property
    def supermimic_mdp(self) -> SuperMimicMdpProtocol: ...


def get_supermimic_mdp(env: Any) -> SuperMimicMdpProtocol:
    """Return the injected task MDP, failing closed for unsupported envs."""
    if not isinstance(env, SupportsSuperMimicProtocol):
        raise TypeError(f"{type(env).__name__} does not provide the SuperMimic MDP contract")
    return env.supermimic_mdp


__all__ = [
    "SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY",
    "SUPERMIMIC_TRANSITION_KEY",
    "State",
    "SuperMimicMdpProtocol",
    "SupportsSuperMimicProtocol",
    "get_supermimic_mdp",
]

"""Batched tensor geometry for SuperMimic (quaternions use wxyz)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.contract import State


def quat_to_mat(q: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    q = F.normalize(q, dim=-1)
    w, x, y, z = q.unbind(-1)
    return torch.stack(
        (
            1 - 2 * (y * y + z * z),
            2 * (x * y - w * z),
            2 * (x * z + w * y),
            2 * (x * y + w * z),
            1 - 2 * (x * x + z * z),
            2 * (y * z - w * x),
            2 * (x * z - w * y),
            2 * (y * z + w * x),
            1 - 2 * (x * x + y * y),
        ),
        dim=-1,
    ).reshape(q.shape[:-1] + (3, 3))


def mat_to_quat(m: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    m00, m01, m02 = m[..., 0, 0], m[..., 0, 1], m[..., 0, 2]
    m10, m11, m12 = m[..., 1, 0], m[..., 1, 1], m[..., 1, 2]
    m20, m21, m22 = m[..., 2, 0], m[..., 2, 1], m[..., 2, 2]
    qw = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(1 + m00 + m11 + m22, min=1e-10)) / 2
    qx = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(1 + m00 - m11 - m22, min=1e-10)) / 2
    qy = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(1 - m00 + m11 - m22, min=1e-10)) / 2
    qz = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(1 - m00 - m11 + m22, min=1e-10)) / 2
    candidates = torch.stack(
        (
            torch.stack(
                (qw, (m21 - m12) / (4 * qw), (m02 - m20) / (4 * qw), (m10 - m01) / (4 * qw)), -1
            ),
            torch.stack(
                ((m21 - m12) / (4 * qx), qx, (m01 + m10) / (4 * qx), (m02 + m20) / (4 * qx)), -1
            ),
            torch.stack(
                ((m02 - m20) / (4 * qy), (m01 + m10) / (4 * qy), qy, (m12 + m21) / (4 * qy)), -1
            ),
            torch.stack(
                ((m10 - m01) / (4 * qz), (m02 + m20) / (4 * qz), (m12 + m21) / (4 * qz), qz), -1
            ),
        ),
        dim=-2,
    )
    indices = torch.stack((qw, qx, qy, qz), -1).argmax(-1)
    q = torch.gather(
        candidates, -2, indices[..., None, None].expand(indices.shape + (1, 4))
    ).squeeze(-2)
    q = F.normalize(q, dim=-1)
    return q * torch.where(q[..., :1] < 0, -1.0, 1.0)


def axis_angle_to_mat(axis: torch.Tensor, angle: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    x, y, z = axis.unbind(-1)
    cos, sin = torch.cos(angle), torch.sin(angle)
    complement = 1 - cos
    return torch.stack(
        (
            cos + x * x * complement,
            x * y * complement - z * sin,
            x * z * complement + y * sin,
            y * x * complement + z * sin,
            cos + y * y * complement,
            y * z * complement - x * sin,
            z * x * complement - y * sin,
            z * y * complement + x * sin,
            cos + z * z * complement,
        ),
        dim=-1,
    ).reshape(angle.shape + (3, 3))


def skew(vector: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    x, y, z = vector.unbind(-1)
    zero = torch.zeros_like(x)
    return torch.stack((zero, -z, y, z, zero, -x, -y, x, zero), -1).reshape(
        vector.shape[:-1] + (3, 3)
    )


def so3_exp(vector: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    theta2 = (vector * vector).sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    theta = torch.sqrt(theta2 + 1e-12)
    small = theta2 < 1e-8
    a = torch.where(small, 1 - theta2 / 6, torch.sin(theta) / theta)
    b = torch.where(small, 0.5 - theta2 / 24, (1 - torch.cos(theta)) / (theta2 + 1e-12))
    skew_matrix = skew(vector)
    eye = torch.eye(3, device=vector.device, dtype=vector.dtype).expand(skew_matrix.shape)
    return eye + a[..., None] * skew_matrix + b[..., None] * (skew_matrix @ skew_matrix)


def quat_to_rotvec(q: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    q = q * torch.where(q[..., :1] < 0, -1.0, 1.0)
    vector = q[..., 1:]
    sin_half = torch.linalg.norm(vector, dim=-1, keepdim=True)
    half = torch.atan2(sin_half, q[..., :1])
    scale = torch.where(
        sin_half < 1e-6,
        2.0 / torch.clamp(q[..., :1], min=1e-6),
        2 * half / (sin_half + 1e-12),
    )
    return vector * scale


def so3_log(rotation: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    return quat_to_rotvec(mat_to_quat(rotation))


def rot_angle_sq(first: torch.Tensor, second: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    trace = (first * second).sum((-1, -2))
    cosine = torch.clamp((trace - 1) / 2, -1 + 1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)
    return torch.acos(cosine) ** 2


def yaw_mat(rotation: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    forward_x, forward_y = rotation[..., 0, 0], rotation[..., 1, 0]
    norm = torch.sqrt(forward_x * forward_x + forward_y * forward_y + 1e-9)
    cos, sin = forward_x / norm, forward_y / norm
    zero, one = torch.zeros_like(cos), torch.ones_like(cos)
    return torch.stack((cos, -sin, zero, sin, cos, zero, zero, zero, one), -1).reshape(
        rotation.shape[:-2] + (3, 3)
    )


def mat_to_6d(rotation: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    return rotation[..., :, :2].reshape(rotation.shape[:-2] + (6,))


def quat_slerp(first: torch.Tensor, second: torch.Tensor, t: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    dot = (first * second).sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    second = torch.where(dot < 0, -second, second)
    dot = dot.abs().clamp(max=1.0)
    theta = torch.acos(dot)
    sin = torch.sin(theta)
    t = t[..., None]
    first_weight = torch.where(sin < 1e-6, 1 - t, torch.sin((1 - t) * theta) / (sin + 1e-12))
    second_weight = torch.where(sin < 1e-6, t, torch.sin(t * theta) / (sin + 1e-12))
    return F.normalize(first_weight * first + second_weight * second, dim=-1)


def flatten_state(state: State) -> torch.Tensor:
    if len(state) != 7:
        raise ValueError("v3 requires six physical state parts plus explicit dynamic aux54")
    position, rotation, lin_vel, ang_vel, joint_pos, joint_vel, aux = state
    if joint_pos.shape[-1] != 30 or joint_vel.shape[-1] != 30 or aux.shape[-1] != 54:
        raise ValueError("v3 physical30/aux54 layout mismatch")
    return torch.cat((position, mat_to_quat(rotation), lin_vel, ang_vel, joint_pos, joint_vel, aux), -1)


def unflatten_state(value: torch.Tensor) -> State:
    if value.shape[-1] != 127:
        raise ValueError("v3 packed state is physical73 plus explicit dynamic54; legacy73 is not a resume")
    return (
        value[..., 0:3], quat_to_mat(value[..., 3:7]),
        value[..., 7:10], value[..., 10:13],
        value[..., 13:43], value[..., 43:73], value[..., 73:127],
    )


def invariant_features(state: State) -> torch.Tensor:
    position, rotation, lin_vel, ang_vel, joint_pos, joint_vel = state
    heading = yaw_mat(rotation)
    rotation_t = rotation.transpose(-1, -2)
    return torch.cat(
        (
            position[..., 2:3],
            mat_to_6d(heading.transpose(-1, -2) @ rotation),
            (rotation_t @ lin_vel.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1),
            (rotation_t @ ang_vel.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1),
            joint_pos,
            joint_vel,
        ),
        -1,
    )


__all__ = [
    "axis_angle_to_mat",
    "flatten_state",
    "invariant_features",
    "mat_to_6d",
    "mat_to_quat",
    "quat_slerp",
    "quat_to_mat",
    "quat_to_rotvec",
    "rot_angle_sq",
    "skew",
    "so3_exp",
    "so3_log",
    "unflatten_state",
    "yaw_mat",
]
