"""SuperMimic training over the injected environment and MDP protocols."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import os
import time
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, cast

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from typing import Any as EnvProtocol  # annotation-only dependency; our real Isaac bridge implements the protocol

from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.contract import (
    SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY,
    SUPERMIMIC_TRANSITION_KEY,
    State,
    get_supermimic_mdp,
)
from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.logging import SuperMimicLogger

from . import networks
from .geometry import unflatten_state
from .networks import Critic, Policy, mlp
from .augmented_wheel_leg_world_model_v1 import AugmentedWheelLegWorldModel as WorldModel
from .replay import ReplayBuffer


@dataclass
class SuperMimicConfig:
    iterations: int = 320_000
    buffer_steps: int = 1000
    utd: float = 0.4
    horizon: int = 32
    goal_frames: int = 10
    hist: int = 10
    z_dim: int = 32
    wm_hidden: tuple[int, ...] = (2048, 2048, 2048)
    policy_hidden: tuple[int, ...] = (2048, 1024, 512, 256)
    critic_hidden: tuple[int, ...] = (256, 128)
    wm_window: int = 8
    wm_batches: int = 10
    wm_batch: int = 2048
    pi_batch: int = 4096
    lr: float = 2e-4
    gamma: float = 0.99
    lam: float = 0.95
    action_noise: float = 0.1
    critic_epochs: int = 5
    critic_minibatches: int = 4
    no_critic: bool = False
    warmup_steps: int = 64
    wm_only_iters: int = 500
    lr_decay_iters: int = 250_000
    ckpt_every: int = 2000
    log_every: int = 50
    log_backend: str = "tensorboard"
    seed: int = 0
    amp: str = "bf16"
    compile: bool = True
    test_hidden_dims: tuple[int, ...] | None = None


_LOG_FIELDS = (
    "iteration",
    "wall_h",
    "sim_steps",
    "wm_loss",
    "wm_err8",
    "pi_loss",
    "v_loss",
    "imag_rew",
    "pi_gradnorm",
    "imag_survival",
    "sim_rew",
    "ep_len",
    "ep_ret_norm",
    "term_frac",
    "it_per_s",
    "lr",
)


def _empty_stats() -> dict[str, list[torch.Tensor]]:
    return {"r": [], "ep_len": [], "ep_ret": [], "term": [], "done": []}


def _set_requires_grad(module: nn.Module, enabled: bool) -> None:
    for parameter in module.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad_(enabled)


class SuperMimicLearner:
    def __init__(
        self,
        env: EnvProtocol,
        cfg: SuperMimicConfig,
        log_dir: str | Path,
        device: str | torch.device | None = None,
        backend: str = "",
    ):
        if cfg.amp not in ("none", "bf16"):
            raise ValueError(f"amp must be 'none' or 'bf16', got {cfg.amp!r}")
        if cfg.utd <= 0:
            raise ValueError("utd must be positive")
        if cfg.test_hidden_dims is not None and not cfg.test_hidden_dims:
            raise ValueError("test_hidden_dims must be nonempty when provided")

        self.env = env
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.backend = backend
        self.log_dir = Path(log_dir)
        self.log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.env_device = torch.device(env.device)
        self.device = self.env_device if device is None else torch.device(device)
        # HOST_BRIDGE backends (e.g. MuJoCo) keep env tensors on CPU while the
        # learner may run on an accelerator (MPS on macOS); when the two differ,
        # the learner works on its own MDP copy and bridges tensors at the env
        # boundary. On CUDA/ROCm the devices always match and nothing changes.
        self._bridged = self.device != self.env_device
        mdp = get_supermimic_mdp(env)
        self.mdp = mdp.to(self.device) if self._bridged else mdp

        # TF32 only pays off for fp32 (amp="none") training: under bf16 autocast
        # the heavy GEMMs already run in bf16, while on ROCm a TF32 bmm over the
        # misaligned strided views produced by unflatten_state triggers per-call
        # rocBLAS kernel selection costing ~100 ms per call.
        torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = cfg.amp == "none"
        torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = True
        torch.manual_seed(cfg.seed)
        networks.AMP_DTYPE = torch.bfloat16 if cfg.amp == "bf16" else None

        self.wm = self._make_world_model().to(self.device)
        self.pi = self._make_policy().to(self.device)
        self.critic = Critic(self._critic_input_dim(), hidden=cfg.critic_hidden).to(self.device)
        if cfg.test_hidden_dims is not None:
            self.critic.net = mlp(self.critic.norm.mean.numel(), cfg.test_hidden_dims, 1).to(
                self.device
            )
        self.opt_wm = torch.optim.Adam(self.wm.parameters(), lr=cfg.lr)
        self.opt_pi = torch.optim.Adam(self.pi.parameters(), lr=cfg.lr, betas=(0.9, 0.95))
        self.opt_v = torch.optim.Adam(self.critic.parameters(), lr=cfg.lr)

        self.buffer = ReplayBuffer(
            cfg.buffer_steps,
            env.num_envs,
            self.device,
            num_joints=self.mdp.num_joints,
            context_dim=self.mdp.context_dim,
            action_dim=getattr(self.mdp,"action_dim",self.mdp.num_joints),
            state_dim=self.mdp.packed_state_dim,
        )
        self.obs_hist = torch.zeros(env.num_envs, cfg.hist, self.mdp.obs_dim, device=self.device)
        self.err_floor = torch.cat(
            (
                torch.full((3,), 3e-3),
                torch.full((3,), 5e-3),
                torch.full((3,), 2e-2),
                torch.full((3,), 5e-2),
                torch.full((self.mdp.num_joints,), 3e-3),
                torch.full((self.mdp.num_joints,), 5e-2),
                torch.full((self.mdp.auxiliary_dim,), 1e-3),
            )
        ).to(self.device)

        self.iteration = 0
        self.sim_steps = 0
        self.wall_time = 0.0
        self.stats = _empty_stats()
        self._wm_rollout_fn = self._compile(self._wm_rollout)
        self._wm_err_fn = self._compile(self._wm_err)
        self._imag_step_fn = self._compile(self._imag_step)
        self._reward_term_fn = self._compile(self._reward_term)
        self._critic_input_fn = self._compile(self._critic_input)

        with (self.log_dir / "args.json").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            json.dump(asdict(cfg), stream, indent=2)

    def _compile(self, function):
        if not self.cfg.compile:
            return function
        if self.device.type == "mps":
            # Inductor Metal codegen is not usable on MPS; keep AOT graph
            # capture without kernel generation.
            return torch.compile(function, backend="aot_eager")
        return torch.compile(function)

    def _make_world_model(self) -> WorldModel:
        kwargs = {
            "num_joints": self.mdp.num_joints,
            "context_dim": self.mdp.context_dim,
            "action_dim": getattr(self.mdp,"action_dim",self.mdp.num_joints),
            "auxiliary_dim": self.mdp.auxiliary_dim,
            "edge_x_m": self.mdp.step_edge_x_m,
            "platform_top_z_m": self.mdp.platform_top_z_m,
        }
        if self.cfg.test_hidden_dims is not None:
            return WorldModel(hidden=self.cfg.test_hidden_dims, **kwargs)
        return WorldModel(hidden=self.cfg.wm_hidden, **kwargs)

    def _make_policy(self) -> Policy:
        goal_dim = self.mdp.goal_frame_dim * self.cfg.goal_frames
        policy = Policy(
            self.mdp.obs_dim,
            goal_dim,
            hist=self.cfg.hist,
            z_dim=self.cfg.z_dim,
            num_joints=getattr(self.mdp,"action_dim",self.mdp.num_joints),
            hidden=self.cfg.policy_hidden,
        )
        if self.cfg.test_hidden_dims is not None:
            enc_output = policy.enc[-1]
            assert isinstance(enc_output, nn.Linear)
            z_dim = enc_output.out_features
            policy.enc = mlp(goal_dim, self.cfg.test_hidden_dims, z_dim)
            policy.dec = mlp(
                z_dim + self.mdp.obs_dim * self.cfg.hist,
                self.cfg.test_hidden_dims,
                getattr(self.mdp,"action_dim",self.mdp.num_joints),
            )
            dec_output = policy.dec[-1]
            assert isinstance(dec_output, nn.Linear)
            with torch.no_grad():
                dec_output.weight.mul_(0.01)
                dec_output.bias.zero_()
        return policy

    def _state_info(self) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        state = self.env.state
        if state is None:
            state = self.env.init_state()
        info: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = state.info[SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY]
        if self._bridged:
            info = {key: value.to(self.device) for key, value in info.items()}
        return info

    def _critic_input_dim(self) -> int:
        info = self._state_info()
        with torch.no_grad():
            features = self.mdp.critic_features(
                unflatten_state(info["s"]),
                info["f"],
                self.mdp.normalize_context(info["xi"]),
            )
        return int(features.shape[-1] + self.mdp.goal_frame_dim * self.cfg.goal_frames)

    def _wm_rollout(
        self, states: torch.Tensor, actions: torch.Tensor, contexts: torch.Tensor
    ) -> State:
        state = unflatten_state(states[:, 0])
        predictions = []
        for index in range(self.cfg.wm_window):
            state = self.wm.step(
                state,
                actions[:, index],
                self.mdp.normalize_context(contexts[:, index]),
            )
            predictions.append(state)
        return tuple(torch.stack([prediction[i] for prediction in predictions], 1) for i in range(7))

    def _wm_err(
        self, predicted: State, next_states: torch.Tensor, mask: torch.Tensor
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        std = torch.maximum(self.wm.out_norm.std(), self.err_floor)
        error = (self.wm.state_error(predicted, unflatten_state(next_states)) / std) ** 2
        error = error.mean(-1)
        return (error * mask).sum() / next_states.shape[0], error[:, -1]

    def _imag_step(
        self,
        state: State,
        obs_hist: torch.Tensor,
        previous_action: torch.Tensor,
        frames: torch.Tensor,
        context: torch.Tensor,
        normalized_context: torch.Tensor,
        goal: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> tuple[State, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        action = torch.clamp(self.pi(obs_hist, goal), -6, 6)
        next_state = self.wm.step(state, action, normalized_context)
        next_frames = self.mdp.advance_reward_frame(state, next_state, frames)
        obs = self.mdp.add_noise(self.mdp.obs(next_state, action, context, next_frames))
        next_hist = torch.cat((obs_hist[:, 1:], obs.unsqueeze(1)), 1)
        next_goal = self.mdp.goal(next_frames, self.cfg.goal_frames)
        return next_state, action, next_hist, next_frames, next_goal

    def _reward_term(
        self,
        state: State,
        frames: torch.Tensor,
        action: torch.Tensor,
        previous_action: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        return self.mdp.reward(state, frames, action, previous_action), self.mdp.soft_termination(
            state, frames
        )

    def _critic_input(
        self,
        state: State,
        frames: torch.Tensor,
        normalized_context: torch.Tensor,
        goal: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.cat((self.mdp.critic_features(state, frames, normalized_context), goal), -1)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def collect(self, n_steps: int) -> None:
        for _ in range(n_steps):
            info = self._state_info()
            state = unflatten_state(info["s"])
            obs = self.mdp.add_noise(self.mdp.obs(state, info["a_prev"], info["xi"], info["f"]))
            fresh = info["ep_step"] == 0
            shifted = torch.cat((self.obs_hist[:, 1:], obs.unsqueeze(1)), 1)
            self.obs_hist = torch.where(
                fresh[:, None, None], obs.unsqueeze(1).expand_as(self.obs_hist), shifted
            )
            goal = self.mdp.goal(info["f"], self.cfg.goal_frames)
            self.pi.obs_norm.update(obs)
            self.pi.goal_norm.update(goal)
            if hasattr(self.env,"before_action"):
                self.env.before_action()
            action = self.pi(self.obs_hist, goal)
            action = torch.clamp(action + self.cfg.action_noise * torch.randn_like(action), -20, 20)
            if self._bridged:
                step_state = self.env.step(action.to(self.env_device))
            else:
                step_state = self.env.step(action)
            transition = step_state.info[SUPERMIMIC_TRANSITION_KEY]
            if self._bridged:
                transition = {key: value.to(self.device) for key, value in transition.items()}
            self.buffer.add(transition)
            initial = unflatten_state(transition["s"])
            next_state = unflatten_state(transition["s1"])
            self.wm.in_norm.update(
                self.wm.inputs(
                    initial,
                    transition["a"],
                    self.mdp.normalize_context(transition["xi"]),
                )
            )
            self.wm.out_norm.update(self.wm.targets(initial, next_state))
            self.sim_steps += self.env.num_envs
            self.stats["r"].append(transition["r"].mean() / self.mdp.max_step_reward)
            if "ep_len" in transition:
                self.stats["ep_len"].append(transition["ep_len"].mean())
                self.stats["ep_ret"].append(transition["ep_ret_norm"].mean())
                self.stats["term"].append(transition["term"].float().sum())
                self.stats["done"].append(transition["done"].float().sum())

    def wm_update(self) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        window = self.buffer.sample_windows(self.cfg.wm_batch, self.cfg.wm_window)
        predicted = self._wm_rollout_fn(window["s"], window["a"], window["xi"])
        loss, last_error = self._wm_err_fn(predicted, window["s1"], window["mask"])
        self.opt_wm.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        loss.backward()
        nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.wm.parameters(), 1.0)
        self.opt_wm.step()
        mask = window["mask"][:, -1]
        mean_last_error = (last_error.detach() * mask).sum() / mask.sum().clamp(min=1)
        return loss.detach() / self.cfg.wm_window, mean_last_error

    def _sample_valid_starts(
        self, batch_size: int
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, torch.Tensor], dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
        starts = []
        histories = []
        count = 0
        while count < batch_size:
            start, history = self.buffer.sample_starts(2 * batch_size, self.cfg.hist)
            valid = self.mdp.valid_imagination_start(start["f"], self.cfg.horizon)
            indices = valid.nonzero(as_tuple=False).squeeze(-1)[: batch_size - count]
            starts.append({key: value[indices] for key, value in start.items()})
            histories.append({key: value[indices] for key, value in history.items()})
            count += len(indices)

        def concatenate(parts: list[dict[str, torch.Tensor]]) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
            return {key: torch.cat([part[key] for part in parts]) for key in parts[0]}

        return concatenate(starts), concatenate(histories)

    def policy_update(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        start, history = self._sample_valid_starts(self.cfg.pi_batch)
        state = unflatten_state(start["s"])
        context, frames, previous_action = start["xi"], start["f"], start["a_prev"]
        normalized_context = self.mdp.normalize_context(context)
        with torch.no_grad():
            obs_hist = self.mdp.obs(
                unflatten_state(history["s"]),
                history["a_prev"],
                history["xi"],
                history["f"],
            )
        obs_hist = self.mdp.add_noise(obs_hist)
        _set_requires_grad(self.wm, False)
        _set_requires_grad(self.critic, False)

        batch_size = self.cfg.pi_batch
        initial_goal = self.mdp.goal(frames, self.cfg.goal_frames)
        goal = initial_goal
        initial_state = state
        states, actions, previous_actions, all_frames, goals = [], [], [], [], []
        try:
            for _ in range(self.cfg.horizon):
                previous_actions.append(previous_action)
                state, previous_action, obs_hist, frames, goal = self._imag_step_fn(
                    state,
                    obs_hist,
                    previous_action,
                    frames,
                    context,
                    normalized_context,
                    goal,
                )
                states.append(state)
                actions.append(previous_action)
                all_frames.append(frames)
                goals.append(goal)

            def flatten(parts: list[torch.Tensor]) -> torch.Tensor:
                return torch.stack(parts).flatten(0, 1)

            all_states = cast(
                State, tuple(flatten([item[index] for item in states]) for index in range(7))
            )
            flat_frames = flatten(all_frames)
            rewards, dones = self._reward_term_fn(
                all_states, flat_frames, flatten(actions), flatten(previous_actions)
            )
            rewards = rewards.view(self.cfg.horizon, batch_size)
            dones = dones.view(self.cfg.horizon, batch_size)
            alive = torch.cumprod(1 - dones, 0)
            continuation = torch.cat((torch.ones(1, batch_size, device=self.device), alive[:-1]), 0)
            discount = self.cfg.gamma ** torch.arange(
                self.cfg.horizon, device=self.device, dtype=torch.float32
            ).unsqueeze(-1)
            objective = (discount * continuation * rewards).sum(0)
            survival = alive[-1]
            reward_sum = (continuation * rewards).detach().sum(0)
            detached_rewards, detached_dones = rewards.detach(), dones.detach()
            if not self.cfg.no_critic:
                objective = objective + (self.cfg.gamma**self.cfg.horizon) * survival * self.critic(
                    self._critic_input_fn(state, frames, normalized_context, goal)
                )
            loss = -objective.mean()
            self.opt_pi.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            loss.backward()
            grad_norm = nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.pi.parameters(), 1.0)
            nn.utils.clip_grad_value_(self.pi.parameters(), 0.1)
            if torch.isfinite(grad_norm):
                self.opt_pi.step()
        finally:
            _set_requires_grad(self.wm, True)
            _set_requires_grad(self.critic, True)

        value_loss = torch.zeros((), device=self.device)
        if not self.cfg.no_critic:
            with torch.no_grad():
                critic_states = cast(
                    State,
                    tuple(
                        torch.cat(
                            (
                                initial_state[index].unsqueeze(0),
                                torch.stack([item[index].detach() for item in states]),
                            ),
                            0,
                        ).flatten(0, 1)
                        for index in range(7)
                    ),
                )
                critic_frames = torch.cat(
                    (start["f"].unsqueeze(0), torch.stack(all_frames)), 0
                ).flatten()
                critic_goals = torch.cat(
                    (initial_goal.unsqueeze(0), torch.stack(goals)), 0
                ).flatten(0, 1)
                critic_context = (
                    normalized_context.unsqueeze(0)
                    .expand(self.cfg.horizon + 1, -1, -1)
                    .flatten(0, 1)
                )
                critic_input = self._critic_input_fn(
                    critic_states, critic_frames, critic_context, critic_goals
                ).view(self.cfg.horizon + 1, batch_size, -1)
            self.critic.norm.update(critic_input)
            with torch.no_grad():
                values = self.critic(critic_input)
                target = values[-1]
                targets = []
                for index in range(self.cfg.horizon - 1, -1, -1):
                    target = detached_rewards[index] + self.cfg.gamma * (
                        1 - detached_dones[index]
                    ) * ((1 - self.cfg.lam) * values[index + 1] + self.cfg.lam * target)
                    targets.append(target)
                targets = torch.stack(targets[::-1])
            flat_input = critic_input[:-1].reshape(-1, critic_input.shape[-1])
            flat_targets = targets.reshape(-1)
            num_samples = flat_input.shape[0]
            minibatch_size = num_samples // self.cfg.critic_minibatches
            if minibatch_size == 0:
                raise ValueError("critic_minibatches exceeds the number of imagined transitions")
            for _ in range(self.cfg.critic_epochs):
                permutation = torch.randperm(num_samples, device=self.device)
                for index in range(self.cfg.critic_minibatches):
                    indices = permutation[index * minibatch_size : (index + 1) * minibatch_size]
                    value_loss = (
                        (self.critic(flat_input[indices]) - flat_targets[indices]) ** 2
                    ).mean()
                    self.opt_v.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                    value_loss.backward()
                    nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.critic.parameters(), 1.0)
                    self.opt_v.step()

        imagined_reward = (reward_sum / self.cfg.horizon / self.mdp.max_step_reward).mean()
        return (
            loss.detach(),
            value_loss.detach(),
            imagined_reward,
            grad_norm.detach(),
            survival.mean().detach(),
        )

    def lr_at(self, iteration: int) -> float:
        if iteration >= self.cfg.lr_decay_iters:
            return self.cfg.lr * 0.03
        return self.cfg.lr * (
            0.03 + 0.97 * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * iteration / self.cfg.lr_decay_iters))
        )

    def _checkpoint(self, include_optimizers: bool) -> dict[str, Any]:
        checkpoint = {
            "wm": self.wm.state_dict(),
            "pi": self.pi.state_dict(),
            "iteration": self.iteration,
            "wall_time": self.wall_time,
            "sim_steps": self.sim_steps,
            "args": asdict(self.cfg),
            "saved_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        }
        if include_optimizers:
            checkpoint.update(
                {
                    "critic": self.critic.state_dict(),
                    "opt_wm": self.opt_wm.state_dict(),
                    "opt_pi": self.opt_pi.state_dict(),
                    "opt_v": self.opt_v.state_dict(),
                }
            )
        return checkpoint

    def save(self, path: str | Path | None = None, include_optimizers: bool = True) -> Path:
        destination = Path(path) if path is not None else self.log_dir / "last.pt"
        destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        temporary = destination.with_name(destination.name + ".tmp")
        torch.save(self._checkpoint(include_optimizers), temporary)
        os.replace(temporary, destination)
        return destination

    def load(self, path: str | Path) -> None:
        checkpoint = torch.load(path, map_location=self.device, weights_only=True)
        required = {"critic", "opt_wm", "opt_pi", "opt_v"}
        missing = sorted(required - checkpoint.keys())
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(
                f"SuperMimic resume requires a full last.pt checkpoint; missing {missing} in {path}"
            )
        self.wm.load_state_dict(checkpoint["wm"])
        self.pi.load_state_dict(checkpoint["pi"])
        self.critic.load_state_dict(checkpoint["critic"])
        self.opt_wm.load_state_dict(checkpoint["opt_wm"])
        self.opt_pi.load_state_dict(checkpoint["opt_pi"])
        self.opt_v.load_state_dict(checkpoint["opt_v"])
        self.iteration = checkpoint["iteration"]
        self.wall_time = checkpoint["wall_time"]
        self.sim_steps = checkpoint["sim_steps"]

    @staticmethod
    def _mean(values: list[torch.Tensor]) -> float:
        return float(sum(float(value) for value in values) / max(len(values), 1))

    def train(self, resume: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, float | int | bool]:
        if resume is not None:
            self.load(resume)
        start_iteration = self.iteration
        self.stats = _empty_stats()
        logger = SuperMimicLogger(
            env_name=type(self.env).__name__,
            backend=self.backend,
            max_iterations=self.cfg.iterations,
            wm_only_iters=self.cfg.wm_only_iters,
            start_iteration=start_iteration,
            num_envs=self.env.num_envs,
            log_dir=str(self.log_dir),
            log_backend=self.cfg.log_backend,
        )
        logger.start(status="Warming up...")
        try:
            if start_iteration == 0:
                self.collect(self.cfg.warmup_steps)
            required_steps = max(self.cfg.hist, self.cfg.wm_window)
            if start_iteration > 0:
                required_steps = max(required_steps, self.cfg.warmup_steps)
            if self.buffer.size < required_steps:
                self.collect(required_steps - self.buffer.size)
            logger.log_buffer_fill(self.buffer.size, self.cfg.buffer_steps)
            logger.start_training_timer()

            log_path = self.log_dir / "train_log.csv"
            with log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as log_file:
                if start_iteration == 0:
                    log_file.write(",".join(_LOG_FIELDS) + "\n")
                train_start = time.time() - self.wall_time
                log_start = time.time()
                step_accumulator = 0.0
                aggregate: dict[str, list[torch.Tensor]] = {
                    key: [] for key in ("wm", "wm8", "pi", "v", "ir", "gn", "surv")
                }
                last_metrics: dict[str, float | int] = {}
                for iteration in range(start_iteration, self.cfg.iterations):
                    if self.cfg.compile and iteration == start_iteration:
                        logger.log_status("Compiling world model (one-off, slow on first run)")
                    if self.cfg.compile and iteration == self.cfg.wm_only_iters:
                        logger.log_status("Compiling policy update (one-off, slow on first run)")
                    iteration_start = time.time()
                    learning_rate = self.lr_at(iteration)
                    for optimizer in (self.opt_wm, self.opt_pi, self.opt_v):
                        for group in optimizer.param_groups:
                            group["lr"] = learning_rate
                    step_accumulator += 1.0 / self.cfg.utd
                    collect_steps = int(step_accumulator)
                    step_accumulator -= collect_steps
                    stats_mark = len(self.stats["r"])
                    self.collect(collect_steps)
                    train_phase_start = time.time()
                    wm_loss = torch.zeros((), device=self.device)
                    wm_error = torch.zeros((), device=self.device)
                    for _ in range(self.cfg.wm_batches):
                        wm_loss, wm_error = self.wm_update()
                    aggregate["wm"].append(wm_loss)
                    aggregate["wm8"].append(wm_error)
                    pi_loss: float | None = None
                    v_loss: float | None = None
                    imag_rew: float | None = None
                    pi_gradnorm: float | None = None
                    imag_survival: float | None = None
                    if self.native88_migration.allow_policy_update(iteration):
                        policy_loss, value_loss, imagined_reward, grad_norm, survival = (
                            self.policy_update()
                        )
                        aggregate["pi"].append(policy_loss)
                        aggregate["v"].append(value_loss)
                        aggregate["ir"].append(imagined_reward)
                        aggregate["gn"].append(grad_norm)
                        aggregate["surv"].append(survival)
                        pi_loss = float(policy_loss)
                        v_loss = float(value_loss)
                        imag_rew = float(imagined_reward)
                        pi_gradnorm = float(grad_norm)
                        imag_survival = float(survival)
                    train_time = time.time() - train_phase_start
                    self.iteration = iteration + 1
                    self.wall_time = time.time() - train_start
                    iteration_time = time.time() - iteration_start

                    iter_rewards = self.stats["r"][stats_mark:]
                    mean_reward = self._mean(iter_rewards) if iter_rewards else None
                    ep_len = self._mean(self.stats["ep_len"]) if self.stats["ep_len"] else None
                    ep_ret_norm = self._mean(self.stats["ep_ret"]) if self.stats["ep_ret"] else None
                    total_done = sum(float(value) for value in self.stats["done"])
                    term_frac = (
                        sum(float(value) for value in self.stats["term"]) / total_done
                        if total_done > 0
                        else None
                    )
                    logger.log_step(
                        self.iteration,
                        wm_loss=float(wm_loss),
                        wm_err8=float(wm_error),
                        pi_loss=pi_loss,
                        v_loss=v_loss,
                        imag_rew=imag_rew,
                        imag_survival=imag_survival,
                        pi_gradnorm=pi_gradnorm,
                        lr=learning_rate,
                        sim_rew=mean_reward,
                        ep_len=ep_len,
                        ep_ret_norm=ep_ret_norm,
                        term_frac=term_frac,
                        iter_sim_steps=collect_steps * self.env.num_envs,
                        collect_time=train_phase_start - iteration_start,
                        learn_time=train_time,
                        iteration_time=iteration_time,
                    )

                    if self.iteration % self.cfg.log_every == 0:
                        iterations_per_second = self.cfg.log_every / (time.time() - log_start)
                        log_start = time.time()
                        term_frac = float(
                            sum(float(value) for value in self.stats["term"])
                            / max(sum(float(value) for value in self.stats["done"]), 1)
                        )
                        row = [
                            self.iteration,
                            self.wall_time / 3600,
                            self.sim_steps,
                            self._mean(aggregate["wm"]),
                            self._mean(aggregate["wm8"]),
                            self._mean(aggregate["pi"]),
                            self._mean(aggregate["v"]),
                            self._mean(aggregate["ir"]),
                            self._mean(aggregate["gn"]),
                            self._mean(aggregate["surv"]),
                            self._mean(self.stats["r"]),
                            self._mean(self.stats["ep_len"]),
                            self._mean(self.stats["ep_ret"]),
                            term_frac,
                            iterations_per_second,
                            learning_rate,
                        ]
                        log_file.write(
                            ",".join(
                                f"{value:.6g}" if isinstance(value, float) else str(value)
                                for value in row
                            )
                            + "\n"
                        )
                        log_file.flush()
                        last_metrics = dict(zip(_LOG_FIELDS, row, strict=True))
                        self.stats = _empty_stats()
                        aggregate = {key: [] for key in aggregate}

                    if (
                        self.iteration % self.cfg.ckpt_every == 0
                        or self.iteration == self.cfg.iterations
                    ):
                        self.wall_time = time.time() - train_start
                        self.save()
                        numbered = self.save(
                            self.log_dir / f"ckpt_{self.iteration:06d}.pt",
                            include_optimizers=False,
                        )
                        logger.log_save(str(numbered))

            self.wall_time = time.time() - train_start
            logger.finish()
            return {
                "completed": self.iteration >= self.cfg.iterations,
                "iteration": self.iteration,
                "sim_steps": self.sim_steps,
                "wall_time": self.wall_time,
                **last_metrics,
            }
        finally:
            logger.close()


__all__ = ["SuperMimicConfig", "SuperMimicLearner"]

# Adapted from UniLab (Apache-2.0) unilab/rl/logging/offpolicy.py
"""SuperMimic training logger: Rich live panel plus TensorBoard scalars."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
import time
from collections import deque
from typing import Any

from rich import box
from rich.console import Console, Group
from rich.live import Live
from rich.panel import Panel
from rich.progress import BarColumn, Progress, TextColumn
from rich.table import Table
from rich.text import Text

_METRIC_LABELS = (
    ("wm_loss", "wm_loss"),
    ("wm_err8", "wm_err8"),
    ("pi_loss", "pi_loss"),
    ("v_loss", "v_loss"),
    ("imag_rew", "imag_rew"),
    ("imag_survival", "imag_survival"),
    ("pi_gradnorm", "pi_gradnorm"),
    ("lr", "lr"),
)

_SPARKLINE_TICKS = "▁▂▃▄▅▆▇█"
_SPARKLINE_ASCII_TICKS = " .:-=+*#%@"
_RATE_WINDOW = 50

_PHASE_PREFILL = "prefill"
_PHASE_WM = "wm"
_PHASE_JOINT = "joint"
_PHASE_LABELS = {_PHASE_WM: "WM-only", _PHASE_JOINT: "Joint"}


def _device_info_line() -> str:
    try:
        from unilab.utils.device import get_device_info_line

        return str(get_device_info_line())
    except Exception:
        return ""


def _fmt_time(seconds: float) -> str:
    if seconds < 60:
        return f"{seconds:.0f}s"
    m, s = divmod(int(seconds), 60)
    if m < 60:
        return f"{m}m{s:02d}s"
    h, m = divmod(m, 60)
    return f"{h}h{m:02d}m{s:02d}s"


def _fmt_number(value: float) -> str:
    if abs(value) == 0:
        return "0"
    if abs(value) >= 1e6:
        return f"{value:.2e}"
    if abs(value) >= 100:
        return f"{value:.1f}"
    if abs(value) >= 1:
        return f"{value:.3f}"
    if abs(value) >= 0.001:
        return f"{value:.4f}"
    return f"{value:.2e}"


def _console_supports_unicode() -> bool:
    encoding = getattr(sys.stdout, "encoding", None) or "utf-8"
    try:
        "⏱ ▲ ▼ ━ ▁█".encode(encoding)
    except UnicodeEncodeError:
        return False
    return True


def _tb_proto_modules() -> tuple[Any, Any]:
    """Import tensorboard's protobuf modules as ``Any``.

    The generated modules define their messages through reflection, which
    static checkers cannot see; returning them as ``Any`` keeps attribute
    access clean without per-line suppressions.
    """
    from tensorboard.compat.proto import event_pb2, summary_pb2

    return event_pb2, summary_pb2


class SuperMimicLogger:
    """Rich live panel and TensorBoard backend with SuperMimic metric names."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        env_name: str,
        backend: str = "",
        max_iterations: int,
        wm_only_iters: int = 0,
        start_iteration: int = 0,
        num_envs: int,
        log_dir: str,
        log_backend: str = "tensorboard",
        refresh_per_second: int = 1,
    ) -> None:
        backend_mode = log_backend.lower()
        if backend_mode not in ("tensorboard", "none", "no_print"):
            raise ValueError(
                f"log_backend must be 'tensorboard', 'none', or 'no_print', got {log_backend!r}"
            )
        self.algo_name = "SuperMimic"
        self.env_name = env_name
        self.backend = backend
        self.max_iterations = max_iterations
        self.num_envs = num_envs
        self.wm_only_iters = min(max(int(wm_only_iters), 0), max_iterations)
        self._device_info = _device_info_line()

        self._no_print = backend_mode == "no_print"
        self._unicode_console = _console_supports_unicode()
        self._console = Console(force_terminal=False if not self._unicode_console else None)
        self._interactive = self._console.is_terminal and not self._no_print
        self._refresh_rate = refresh_per_second
        self._live: Live | None = None
        self._last_live_refresh_time: float | None = None

        self._start_time = 0.0
        self._iteration = 0
        self._status = ""
        self._phase = _PHASE_PREFILL
        self._metrics: dict[str, float | None] = {key: None for key, _ in _METRIC_LABELS}
        self._reward_history: deque[float] = deque(maxlen=200)
        self._ep_len: float | None = None
        self._ep_ret_norm: float | None = None
        self._term_frac: float | None = None
        self._collect_time = 0.0
        self._learn_time = 0.0
        self._iteration_time = 0.0
        self._iteration_times: deque[float] = deque(maxlen=_RATE_WINDOW)
        self._iteration_steps: deque[int] = deque(maxlen=_RATE_WINDOW)
        self._phase_times: dict[str, deque[float]] = {
            _PHASE_WM: deque(maxlen=_RATE_WINDOW),
            _PHASE_JOINT: deque(maxlen=_RATE_WINDOW),
        }
        self._last_save = ""

        # One bar per training phase, each with its own ETA: the WM-only and
        # joint phases have very different per-iteration costs, so a single
        # shared bar/ETA is misleading. Buffer prefill stays a status line.
        self._progress = Progress(
            TextColumn("[yellow]{task.description} {task.completed}/{task.total}"),
            BarColumn(bar_width=None),
            TextColumn("[progress.percentage]{task.percentage:>3.0f}%"),
            TextColumn("[magenta]{task.fields[eta]}"),
            expand=True,
        )
        wm_total = self.wm_only_iters
        joint_total = max_iterations - self.wm_only_iters
        self._wm_task = (
            self._progress.add_task(
                _PHASE_LABELS[_PHASE_WM],
                total=wm_total,
                completed=min(start_iteration, wm_total),
                eta="",
            )
            if wm_total > 0
            else None
        )
        self._joint_task = (
            self._progress.add_task(
                _PHASE_LABELS[_PHASE_JOINT],
                total=joint_total,
                completed=max(start_iteration - self.wm_only_iters, 0),
                eta="",
            )
            if joint_total > 0
            else None
        )

        self._tb_writer: Any | None = None
        self._finished = False
        self._closed = False
        if backend_mode == "tensorboard" and log_dir:
            self._init_tensorboard(log_dir)

    def _init_tensorboard(self, log_dir: str) -> None:
        try:
            from torch.utils.tensorboard import SummaryWriter
        except ImportError:
            if not self._no_print:
                self._console.print("[yellow]tensorboard not installed[/]")
            return
        os.makedirs(log_dir, exist_ok=True)
        self._tb_writer = SummaryWriter(log_dir=log_dir)
        if not self._no_print:
            self._console.print(f"[dim]TensorBoard logging to: {log_dir}[/]")

    def start(self, *, status: str = "Warming up...") -> None:
        self._start_time = time.time()
        self._status = status
        if self._no_print:
            return
        if self._interactive:
            if self._live is None:
                self._live = Live(
                    self._build_display(),
                    console=self._console,
                    auto_refresh=False,
                    transient=False,
                )
                self._live.start(refresh=True)
        else:
            if self._device_info:
                self._console.print(self._device_info, style="dim")
            self._console.print(status, style="dim")

    def start_training_timer(self) -> float:
        """Restart the measured training window after warm-up collection."""
        self._start_time = time.time()
        return self._start_time

    def log_status(self, status: str) -> None:
        self._status = status
        if self._no_print:
            return
        if not self._interactive:
            self._console.print(status, style="dim")
        self._refresh()

    def log_buffer_fill(self, current: int, target: int) -> None:
        percent = current / max(target, 1) * 100
        self.log_status(f"Replay buffer: {current:,}/{target:,} steps ({percent:.0f}%)")

    def log_save(self, path: str) -> None:
        self._last_save = path
        self.log_status(f"Saved checkpoint: {path}")

    def log_step(
        self,
        iteration: int,
        *,
        wm_loss: float,
        wm_err8: float,
        lr: float,
        pi_loss: float | None = None,
        v_loss: float | None = None,
        imag_rew: float | None = None,
        imag_survival: float | None = None,
        pi_gradnorm: float | None = None,
        sim_rew: float | None = None,
        ep_len: float | None = None,
        ep_ret_norm: float | None = None,
        term_frac: float | None = None,
        iter_sim_steps: int = 0,
        collect_time: float = 0.0,
        learn_time: float = 0.0,
        iteration_time: float = 0.0,
    ) -> None:
        self._iteration = iteration
        self._metrics["wm_loss"] = wm_loss
        self._metrics["wm_err8"] = wm_err8
        self._metrics["lr"] = lr
        if pi_loss is not None:
            self._metrics["pi_loss"] = pi_loss
        if v_loss is not None:
            self._metrics["v_loss"] = v_loss
        if imag_rew is not None:
            self._metrics["imag_rew"] = imag_rew
        if imag_survival is not None:
            self._metrics["imag_survival"] = imag_survival
        if pi_gradnorm is not None:
            self._metrics["pi_gradnorm"] = pi_gradnorm
        if sim_rew is not None:
            self._reward_history.append(sim_rew)
        if ep_len is not None:
            self._ep_len = ep_len
        if ep_ret_norm is not None:
            self._ep_ret_norm = ep_ret_norm
        if term_frac is not None:
            self._term_frac = term_frac
        self._collect_time = collect_time
        self._learn_time = learn_time
        self._iteration_time = iteration_time
        # `iteration` is the 1-based completed count; the 0-based loop counter
        # enters the joint phase at cfg.wm_only_iters.
        phase = _PHASE_JOINT if iteration > self.wm_only_iters else _PHASE_WM
        self._phase = phase
        if iteration_time > 0:
            self._iteration_times.append(iteration_time)
            self._iteration_steps.append(iter_sim_steps)
            self._phase_times[phase].append(iteration_time)
        self._status = _PHASE_LABELS[phase]
        if self._wm_task is not None:
            self._progress.update(
                self._wm_task,
                completed=min(iteration, self.wm_only_iters),
                eta=self._phase_eta(_PHASE_WM),
            )
        if self._joint_task is not None:
            self._progress.update(
                self._joint_task,
                completed=max(iteration - self.wm_only_iters, 0),
                eta=self._phase_eta(_PHASE_JOINT),
            )

        scalars: dict[str, float] = {
            "Loss/world_model": wm_loss,
            "Loss/world_model_err8": wm_err8,
            "Train/learning_rate": lr,
            "Perf/iteration_time": iteration_time,
            "Perf/collect_time": collect_time,
            "Perf/learning_time": learn_time,
            "Perf/it_per_s": self._it_per_s,
            "Perf/sim_steps_per_s": self._sim_steps_per_s,
        }
        if pi_loss is not None:
            scalars["Loss/policy"] = pi_loss
        if v_loss is not None:
            scalars["Loss/value"] = v_loss
        if imag_rew is not None:
            scalars["Loss/imagined_reward"] = imag_rew
        if imag_survival is not None:
            scalars["Loss/imagined_survival"] = imag_survival
        if pi_gradnorm is not None:
            scalars["Train/grad_norm"] = pi_gradnorm
        if sim_rew is not None:
            scalars["Reward/sim_step_norm"] = sim_rew
        if ep_len is not None:
            scalars["Episode/length"] = ep_len
        if ep_ret_norm is not None:
            scalars["Episode/return_norm"] = ep_ret_norm
        if term_frac is not None:
            scalars["Episode/terminated_frac"] = term_frac
        self._write_tb_scalars(list(scalars.items()), iteration)
        self._refresh()

    @property
    def _it_per_s(self) -> float:
        total = sum(self._iteration_times)
        return len(self._iteration_times) / total if total > 0 else 0.0

    @property
    def _sim_steps_per_s(self) -> float:
        total = sum(self._iteration_times)
        return sum(self._iteration_steps) / total if total > 0 else 0.0

    def _phase_eta(self, phase: str) -> str:
        """ETA for one phase from that phase's own iteration times."""
        if phase == _PHASE_WM:
            remaining = self.wm_only_iters - min(self._iteration, self.wm_only_iters)
        else:
            remaining = self.max_iterations - max(self._iteration, self.wm_only_iters)
        if remaining <= 0:
            return "done"
        times = self._phase_times[phase]
        if not times:
            return ""
        mean_iter = sum(times) / len(times)
        return f"ETA {_fmt_time(mean_iter * remaining)}"

    def _write_tb_scalars(self, scalars: list[tuple[str, Any]], global_step: int) -> None:
        """Write one iteration's scalars as a single event record.

        Per-scalar ``add_scalar`` costs the writer thread one open/write/close
        per record; batching keeps it to one record per iteration. Falls back
        to per-scalar writes if the batched path is unavailable.
        """
        writer = self._tb_writer
        if writer is None or not scalars:
            return
        try:
            event_pb2, summary_pb2 = _tb_proto_modules()
            event = event_pb2.Event(
                wall_time=time.time(),
                step=global_step,
                summary=summary_pb2.Summary(
                    value=[
                        summary_pb2.Summary.Value(tag=tag, simple_value=float(value))
                        for tag, value in scalars
                    ]
                ),
            )
            writer.file_writer.add_event(event)
        except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError):
            for tag, value in scalars:
                writer.add_scalar(tag, value, global_step)

    def _refresh(self, *, force: bool = False) -> None:
        if self._live is None:
            return
        now = time.time()
        if (
            not force
            and self._refresh_rate > 0
            and self._last_live_refresh_time is not None
            and now - self._last_live_refresh_time < 1.0 / self._refresh_rate
        ):
            return
        self._last_live_refresh_time = now
        self._live.update(self._build_display(), refresh=True)

    def _sparkline(self, width: int = 32) -> str:
        points = list(self._reward_history)[-width:]
        if not points:
            return ""
        ticks = _SPARKLINE_TICKS if self._unicode_console else _SPARKLINE_ASCII_TICKS
        low, high = min(points), max(points)
        if high - low < 1e-12:
            return ticks[0] * len(points)
        scale = (len(ticks) - 1) / (high - low)
        return "".join(ticks[int((point - low) * scale)] for point in points)

    def _build_header(self) -> Text:
        elapsed = time.time() - self._start_time if self._start_time else 0.0
        fields: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
            (f"{'⏱' if self._unicode_console else 'time'} {_fmt_time(elapsed)}", "green"),
        ]
        if self._phase in _PHASE_LABELS:
            eta = self._phase_eta(self._phase)
            if eta:
                fields.append((f"{_PHASE_LABELS[self._phase]} {eta}", "bold magenta"))
        if self._it_per_s > 0:
            fields.append((f"{self._it_per_s:.2f} it/s", "bold green"))
        if self._sim_steps_per_s > 0:
            fields.append((f"{self._sim_steps_per_s:,.0f} steps/s", "bold cyan"))
        if self._status:
            fields.append((self._status, "dim italic"))
        header = Text(no_wrap=True, overflow="ellipsis", justify="center")
        for index, (text, style) in enumerate(fields):
            if index > 0:
                header.append(" | ", style="dim")
            header.append(text, style=style)
        return header

    def _build_metrics_table(self) -> Table:
        table = Table(
            box=box.SIMPLE_HEAVY,
            show_header=True,
            show_edge=False,
            header_style="bold cyan",
            expand=True,
            pad_edge=False,
        )
        table.add_column("Metrics", style="white", no_wrap=True)
        table.add_column("Value", style="yellow", justify="right", width=13, no_wrap=True)
        for key, label in _METRIC_LABELS:
            value = self._metrics[key]
            table.add_row(label, "[dim]—[/]" if value is None else _fmt_number(value))
        return table

    def _build_reward_table(self) -> Table:
        table = Table(
            box=box.SIMPLE_HEAVY,
            show_header=True,
            show_edge=False,
            header_style="bold green",
            expand=True,
            pad_edge=False,
        )
        table.add_column("Reward / Episode", style="white", no_wrap=True)
        table.add_column("Value", justify="right", no_wrap=True)
        recent = list(self._reward_history)
        if recent:
            mean_rew = sum(recent[-50:]) / len(recent[-50:])
            peak_rew = max(recent)
            trend = ""
            if len(recent) >= 10:
                old = sum(recent[-20:-10]) / 10
                new = sum(recent[-10:]) / 10
                if new > old * 1.05:
                    trend = "[green]▲[/]" if self._unicode_console else "[green]+[/]"
                elif new < old * 0.95:
                    trend = "[red]▼[/]" if self._unicode_console else "[red]-[/]"
                else:
                    trend = "[yellow]━[/]" if self._unicode_console else "[yellow]=[/]"
            table.add_row(
                f"[bold]sim_rew[/] {trend}",
                f"Mean [bold green]{mean_rew:.4f}[/] / Peak [dim]{peak_rew:.4f}[/]",
            )
            table.add_row("", f"[green]{self._sparkline()}[/]")
        else:
            table.add_row("[dim]Waiting for data...[/]", "")
        table.add_row("ep_len", "[dim]—[/]" if self._ep_len is None else f"{self._ep_len:.1f}")
        table.add_row(
            "ep_ret_norm",
            "[dim]—[/]" if self._ep_ret_norm is None else _fmt_number(self._ep_ret_norm),
        )
        table.add_row(
            "term_frac",
            "[dim]—[/]" if self._term_frac is None else f"{self._term_frac * 100:.1f}%",
        )
        return table

    def _build_timing_table(self) -> Table:
        table = Table(
            box=box.SIMPLE_HEAVY,
            show_header=True,
            show_edge=False,
            header_style="bold blue",
            expand=True,
            pad_edge=False,
        )
        table.add_column("Timing (per iteration)", style="white", no_wrap=True)
        table.add_column("Value", style="yellow", justify="right", width=16, no_wrap=True)

        def _add_phase(label: str, seconds: float) -> None:
            ms = seconds * 1000
            pct = seconds / self._iteration_time * 100.0 if self._iteration_time > 0 else 0.0
            table.add_row(label, f"{ms:>7.1f}ms  {pct:>3.0f}%")

        _add_phase("Collect", self._collect_time)
        _add_phase("Learn", self._learn_time)
        table.add_row("Total", f"{self._iteration_time * 1000:>7.1f}ms  100%")
        return table

    def _build_display(self) -> Panel:
        if self._console.width >= 96:
            grid = Table.grid(expand=True)
            grid.add_column(ratio=1)
            grid.add_column(width=2)
            grid.add_column(ratio=1)
            grid.add_row(self._build_metrics_table(), "", self._build_reward_table())
        else:
            grid = Table.grid(expand=True)
            grid.add_column()
            grid.add_row(self._build_metrics_table())
            grid.add_row(Text(""))
            grid.add_row(self._build_reward_table())
        title = Text()
        if self._unicode_console:
            title.append(" 🚀")
        title.append(f" {self.algo_name} ", style="bold cyan")
        title.append("|", style="dim")
        title.append(f" {self.env_name} ", style="bold white")
        if self.backend:
            title.append("|", style="dim")
            title.append(f" {self.backend} ", style="bold magenta")
        rows = [
            self._progress,
            self._build_header(),
        ]
        if self._device_info:
            rows.append(Text(self._device_info, style="dim", no_wrap=True, overflow="ellipsis"))
        rows.extend(
            [
                Text(""),
                grid,
                Text(""),
                self._build_timing_table(),
            ]
        )
        return Panel(
            Group(*rows),
            title=title,
            border_style="bright_blue",
            padding=(0, 1),
        )

    def _stop_live(self) -> None:
        live = self._live
        if live is None:
            return
        self._live = None
        self._last_live_refresh_time = None
        try:
            live.update(self._build_display(), refresh=False)
        finally:
            try:
                live.stop()
            finally:
                self._console.show_cursor(True)

    def _close_writer(self) -> None:
        if self._tb_writer is not None:
            self._tb_writer.close()
            self._tb_writer = None

    def finish(self, *, title: str = "Training Summary") -> None:
        if self._finished:
            return
        self._finished = True
        if self._live is not None:
            self._stop_live()
        elif not self._no_print:
            self._console.print(self._build_display())
        if not self._no_print:
            elapsed = time.time() - self._start_time if self._start_time else 0.0
            lines = [
                "[bold green]Training complete[/]",
                f"  Algo: [cyan]{self.algo_name}[/] | Env: [cyan]{self.env_name}[/]"
                + (f" | Backend: [magenta]{self.backend}[/]" if self.backend else ""),
                f"  Iterations: [yellow]{self._iteration}[/]/{self.max_iterations}",
                f"  Wall time: [yellow]{_fmt_time(elapsed)}[/]",
            ]
            if self._device_info:
                lines.append(f"  [dim]{self._device_info}[/]")
            final = ", ".join(
                f"{label}={_fmt_number(value)}"
                for key, label in _METRIC_LABELS
                if (value := self._metrics[key]) is not None
            )
            if final:
                lines.append(f"  Final: [yellow]{final}[/]")
            if self._last_save:
                lines.append(f"  Last checkpoint: [dim]{self._last_save}[/]")
            self._console.print()
            self._console.print(
                Panel("\n".join(lines), title=f"[bold]{title}[/]", border_style="green")
            )
        self._close_writer()
        self._closed = True

    def close(self) -> None:
        """Release live terminal state and the TB writer; no-op after finish()."""
        if self._closed:
            return
        try:
            self._stop_live()
        finally:
            try:
                self._close_writer()
            finally:
                self._closed = True


__all__ = ["SuperMimicLogger"]

"""World model, goal-conditioned policy, and critic for SuperMimic."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

import torch
import torch.nn as nn

from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.contract import State

from .geometry import invariant_features, so3_exp, so3_log, yaw_mat
from flat91_mask_aware_goal_conditioning_v1 import masked_goal_conditioning


def mlp(
    in_dim: int,
    hidden: Sequence[int],
    out_dim: int,
    act: type[nn.Module] = nn.ELU,
    layer_norm: bool = False,
) -> nn.Sequential:
    layers: list[nn.Module] = []
    dim = in_dim
    for hidden_dim in hidden:
        layers.append(nn.Linear(dim, hidden_dim))
        if layer_norm:
            layers.append(nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim))
        layers.append(act())
        dim = hidden_dim
    layers.append(nn.Linear(dim, out_dim))
    return nn.Sequential(*layers)


class RunningNorm(nn.Module):
    """Running mean and variance, frozen once ``max_count`` is reached."""

    def __init__(self, dim: int, max_count: float = 2e8, eps: float = 1e-2):
        super().__init__()
        self.mean: torch.Tensor
        self.var: torch.Tensor
        self.count: torch.Tensor
        self.register_buffer("mean", torch.zeros(dim))
        self.register_buffer("var", torch.ones(dim))
        self.register_buffer("count", torch.zeros(()))
        self.max_count = max_count
        self.eps = eps

    @torch.no_grad()
    def update(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        x = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1]).float()
        if self.count >= self.max_count:
            return
        batch_count = x.shape[0]
        batch_mean = x.mean(0)
        batch_var = x.var(0, unbiased=False)
        total = self.count + batch_count
        delta = batch_mean - self.mean
        self.mean += delta * batch_count / total
        self.var = (
            self.var * self.count
            + batch_var * batch_count
            + delta**2 * self.count * batch_count / total
        ) / total
        self.count = total

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return (x - self.mean) / torch.sqrt(self.var + self.eps**2)

    def std(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.sqrt(self.var + self.eps**2)


AMP_DTYPE: torch.dtype | None = None


def run_mlp(net: nn.Module, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Run an MLP under optional autocast, returning fp32 for state arithmetic."""
    if AMP_DTYPE is None:
        return net(x)
    device_type = x.device.type
    if device_type not in ("cuda", "mps"):
        return net(x)
    with torch.autocast(device_type, dtype=AMP_DTYPE):
        return net(x).float()


class WorldModel(nn.Module):
    """Predict frame-local state increments and integrate them in fp32."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        hidden: Sequence[int] = (2048, 2048, 2048),
        num_joints: int = 29,
        context_dim: int = 33,
        action_dim: int | None = None,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.num_joints = num_joints
        self.context_dim = context_dim
        self.in_dim = 13 + 2 * num_joints + (num_joints if action_dim is None else action_dim) + context_dim
        self.out_dim = 12 + 2 * num_joints
        self.net = mlp(self.in_dim, hidden, self.out_dim, act=nn.SiLU, layer_norm=True)
        output = self.net[-1]
        assert isinstance(output, nn.Linear)
        nn.init.zeros_(output.weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(output.bias)
        self.in_norm = RunningNorm(self.in_dim)
        self.out_norm = RunningNorm(self.out_dim, eps=1e-4)

    def inputs(
        self, state: State, action: torch.Tensor, normalized_context: torch.Tensor
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.cat((invariant_features(state), action, normalized_context), -1)

    @staticmethod
    def targets(state: State, next_state: State) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Return frame-local increments from ``state`` to ``next_state``."""
        position, rotation, lin_vel, ang_vel, joint_pos, joint_vel = state
        next_position, next_rotation, next_lin_vel, next_ang_vel, next_joint_pos, next_joint_vel = (
            next_state
        )
        heading = yaw_mat(rotation)
        rotation_t = rotation.transpose(-1, -2)
        return torch.cat(
            (
                (heading.transpose(-1, -2) @ (next_position - position).unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1),
                so3_log(rotation_t @ next_rotation),
                (rotation_t @ (next_lin_vel - lin_vel).unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1),
                (rotation_t @ (next_ang_vel - ang_vel).unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1),
                next_joint_pos - joint_pos,
                next_joint_vel - joint_vel,
            ),
            -1,
        )

    def step(
        self,
        state: State,
        action: torch.Tensor,
        normalized_context: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> State:
        position, rotation, lin_vel, ang_vel, joint_pos, joint_vel = state
        prediction = run_mlp(
            self.net,
            self.in_norm(self.inputs(state, action, normalized_context)),
        )
        increment = prediction * self.out_norm.std() + self.out_norm.mean
        heading = yaw_mat(rotation)
        d_position, d_rotation, d_lin_vel, d_ang_vel, d_joint_pos, d_joint_vel = increment.split(
            (3, 3, 3, 3, self.num_joints, self.num_joints), -1
        )
        next_position = position + (heading @ d_position.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)
        next_rotation = rotation @ so3_exp(d_rotation)
        next_lin_vel = lin_vel + (rotation @ d_lin_vel.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)
        next_ang_vel = ang_vel + (rotation @ d_ang_vel.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)
        return (
            next_position,
            next_rotation,
            next_lin_vel,
            next_ang_vel,
            joint_pos + d_joint_pos,
            joint_vel + d_joint_vel,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def state_error(predicted_state: State, state: State) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Return frame-local errors in the same layout as ``targets``."""
        position, rotation, lin_vel, ang_vel, joint_pos, joint_vel = state
        (
            predicted_position,
            predicted_rotation,
            predicted_lin_vel,
            predicted_ang_vel,
            predicted_joint_pos,
            predicted_joint_vel,
        ) = predicted_state
        heading = yaw_mat(rotation)
        rotation_t = rotation.transpose(-1, -2)
        return torch.cat(
            (
                (heading.transpose(-1, -2) @ (predicted_position - position).unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(
                    -1
                ),
                so3_log(rotation_t @ predicted_rotation),
                (rotation_t @ (predicted_lin_vel - lin_vel).unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1),
                (rotation_t @ (predicted_ang_vel - ang_vel).unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1),
                predicted_joint_pos - joint_pos,
                predicted_joint_vel - joint_vel,
            ),
            -1,
        )


class Policy(nn.Module):
    """Encode a goal and decode it together with an observation history."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        obs_dim: int,
        goal_dim: int,
        hist: int = 10,
        z_dim: int = 32,
        num_joints: int = 29,
        hidden: Sequence[int] = (2048, 1024, 512, 256),
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.hist = hist
        self.enc = mlp(goal_dim, hidden, z_dim)
        self.dec = mlp(z_dim + obs_dim * hist, hidden, num_joints)
        output = self.dec[-1]
        assert isinstance(output, nn.Linear)
        with torch.no_grad():
            output.weight.mul_(0.01)
            output.bias.zero_()
        self.obs_norm = RunningNorm(obs_dim)
        self.goal_norm = RunningNorm(goal_dim)

    def forward(self, obs_hist: torch.Tensor, goal: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Consume ``(batch, hist, obs_dim)`` observations, newest last."""
        latent = torch.tanh(run_mlp(self.enc, masked_goal_conditioning(self.goal_norm(goal), goal)))
        obs = self.obs_norm(obs_hist).flatten(-2)
        return run_mlp(self.dec, torch.cat((latent, obs), -1))


class Critic(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_dim: int, hidden: Sequence[int] = (256, 128)):
        super().__init__()
        self.net = mlp(in_dim, hidden, 1)
        self.norm = RunningNorm(in_dim)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        normalized = self.norm(x)
        conditioned = torch.cat((normalized[..., :-1460], masked_goal_conditioning(normalized[..., -1460:], x[..., -1460:])), -1)
        return run_mlp(self.net, conditioned).squeeze(-1)


__all__ = ["AMP_DTYPE", "Critic", "Policy", "RunningNorm", "WorldModel", "run_mlp"]

"""Fixed-size device-resident replay laid out as ``(time, environment)``."""

from __future__ import annotations

import torch


def _make_fields(
    num_joints: int,
    context_dim: int,
    action_dim: int | None = None,
    state_dim: int | None = None,
) -> dict[str, tuple[int | tuple[()], torch.dtype]]:
    state_dim = 13 + 2 * num_joints if state_dim is None else state_dim
    return {
        "s": (state_dim, torch.float32),
        "s1": (state_dim, torch.float32),
        "a": (num_joints if action_dim is None else action_dim, torch.float32),
        "a_prev": (num_joints if action_dim is None else action_dim, torch.float32),
        "xi": (context_dim, torch.float32),
        "r": ((), torch.float32),
        "f": ((), torch.long),
        "ep_step": ((), torch.int32),
        "done": ((), torch.bool),
    }


FIELDS = _make_fields(29, 33)


class ReplayBuffer:
    def __init__(
        self,
        T: int,
        N: int,
        device: str | torch.device,
        num_joints: int = 29,
        context_dim: int = 33,
        action_dim: int | None = None,
        state_dim: int | None = None,
    ):
        self.T = T
        self.N = N
        self.device = torch.device(device)
        self.fields = _make_fields(num_joints, context_dim, action_dim, state_dim)
        self.data = {}
        for key, (shape, dtype) in self.fields.items():
            field_shape = (T, N) + ((shape,) if isinstance(shape, int) else shape)
            self.data[key] = torch.zeros(field_shape, dtype=dtype, device=self.device)
        self.ptr = 0
        self.size = 0

    def add(self, transition: dict[str, torch.Tensor]) -> None:
        for key in self.fields:
            self.data[key][self.ptr] = transition[key]
        self.ptr = (self.ptr + 1) % self.T
        self.size = min(self.size + 1, self.T)

    def _row(self, age: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Map age-ordered time indices (zero is oldest) to storage rows."""
        return (self.ptr - self.size + age) % self.T

    def sample_windows(self, B: int, k: int) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        """Sample ``k``-step windows and mask transitions after episode ends."""
        if not 1 <= k <= self.size:
            raise ValueError(f"window length must be in [1, {self.size}], got {k}")
        start = torch.randint(0, self.size - k + 1, (B,), device=self.device)
        env = torch.randint(0, self.N, (B,), device=self.device)
        ages = start.unsqueeze(-1) + torch.arange(k, device=self.device)
        rows = self._row(ages)
        envs = env.unsqueeze(-1).expand_as(rows)
        out = {key: self.data[key][rows, envs] for key in ("s", "s1", "a", "xi", "done")}
        done = out["done"].float()
        mask = torch.cumprod(
            torch.cat((torch.ones(B, 1, device=self.device), 1 - done[:, :-1]), -1),
            -1,
        )
        out["mask"] = mask
        return out

    def sample_starts(
        self,
        B: int,
        hist: int,
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, torch.Tensor], dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
        """Sample imagination starts and oldest-first, boundary-padded histories."""
        if self.size == 0:
            raise ValueError("cannot sample from an empty replay buffer")
        if hist < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"history length must be positive, got {hist}")
        age = torch.randint(0, self.size, (B,), device=self.device)
        env = torch.randint(0, self.N, (B,), device=self.device)
        row = self._row(age)
        ep_step = self.data["ep_step"][row, env].long()
        back = torch.arange(hist - 1, -1, -1, device=self.device)
        back = torch.minimum(back.unsqueeze(0), ep_step.unsqueeze(-1))
        back = torch.minimum(back, age.unsqueeze(-1))
        history_rows = self._row(age.unsqueeze(-1) - back)
        history_envs = env.unsqueeze(-1).expand_as(history_rows)
        start = {key: self.data[key][row, env] for key in ("s", "a_prev", "xi", "f")}
        history = {
            key: self.data[key][history_rows, history_envs] for key in ("s", "a_prev", "xi", "f")
        }
        return start, history


__all__ = ["FIELDS", "ReplayBuffer"]

"""New task request clock and command, preserving the exact original270 event truth."""
import types
import torch
from native_wheel_leg_bridge_v4 import NativeWheelLegBridge
from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.contract import SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY
from private_phase_frame_v1 import encode
from jump_process_runtime122_v1 import current_process_code

class FudanStepBridge(NativeWheelLegBridge):
    def __init__(self,*args,**kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args,**kwargs)
        self.own_env_ids=torch.arange(self.num_envs,device=self.device,dtype=torch.long)
        self._real_reward_tracking=None
    def _encode_current_frame(self,state):
        row=state.info[SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY]
        row['f']=encode(row['f'],self.own_env_ids,current_process_code(self.real))
        return state
    def init_state(self):return self._encode_current_frame(super().init_state())
    def step(self,actions):
        result=super().step(actions)
        terms=self.mdp.last_reward_components
        # These tensors come from the actual terminal pre-reset state already
        # used for the native reward, never a second actor or world-model call.
        def means(value):
            return {k:means(v) if isinstance(v,dict) else (v.detach().mean() if v.is_floating_point() or v.is_complex() else v.detach().to(dtype=torch.float32).mean()) for k,v in value.items() if isinstance(v,(dict,torch.Tensor))}
        self._real_reward_tracking=means(terms)
        return self._encode_current_frame(result)
    def reward_tracking_snapshot(self):
        def plain(value):return {k:plain(v) if isinstance(v,dict) else float(v.cpu()) for k,v in value.items()}
        return dict(scope='last_complete_actual_control_mean_over_all4096_not_frozen_skill_evaluation',components=None if self._real_reward_tracking is None else plain(self._real_reward_tracking),step_top_z_m=.27,request_control_interval=[200,2000],physical_success_from_original270_counter_only=True)

def install_fudan_step_request_and_command(env):
    import spin_commands
    from h1_original_directional_runtime import audit as directional_audit
    from h1_external_operator_command_v1 import H1ExternalOperatorCommand
    assert not getattr(env,'_own_fudan_step_task_installed',False)
    phase=env.h1_phase
    def request(this):return (this.age_control>=200)&(this.age_control<2000)
    phase.public_request=types.MethodType(request,phase)
    def publish(this):
        this._mod4_mode_bit=this.h1_phase.public_mode()
        term=this.command_manager.get_term('twist');term.set_manual_evaluation_mode();term.vel_command_b.zero_()
        term.vel_command_b[:,0]=this.h1_phase.public_request().to(term.vel_command_b.dtype)*.5*(~this.h1_phase.goal.success).to(term.vel_command_b.dtype)
    def audit(this):
        result=directional_audit(this);term=this.command_manager.get_term('twist')
        assert isinstance(term,H1ExternalOperatorCommand)
        expected=torch.zeros_like(term.command);expected[:,0]=phase.public_request().to(expected.dtype)*.5*(~phase.goal.success).to(expected.dtype)
        assert torch.equal(term.command,expected)
        result.update(stage='MOD4_Fudan106_real270_20s_task',request_control_interval=[200,2000],approach_command_m_s=.5,zero_yaw_and_lateral=True,original_physical_reset_RNG_unchanged=True,original_true270_cross_footprint_topcontact12_stable200_unchanged=True,public_schedule_explicitly_changed_from100_180=True)
        return result
    env._h1_publish_external_request=types.MethodType(publish,env);spin_commands.audit=audit
    env._own_fudan_step_task_installed=True;env._h1_publish_external_request()
    return audit(env)

"""Build NEW123 typed payload from qualified116; binary hardlinks/metadata only."""
from pathlib import Path
import ast,copy,hashlib,json,os,pprint,runpy,shutil,time,traceback

T=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
L=T/'MOD4_H1_OVERNIGHT_FUDAN_SUPERMIMIC_REPAIR_GOAL_20261027'
A=T/'MOD4_H1_FUDAN270_DIRECT_COMMAND_RESOURCE_V116_20261029/payloads/seed27'
Q=L/'TWO116_DIRECT_COMMAND_FIRST_CLOSING_INITIAL_CPU_SOURCE_V1'
F=L/'native122_jump_process_command_reward_source_v1'
N=T/'MOD4_H1_FUDAN270_QUALIFIED_JUMP_PROCESS_RESOURCE_V123_20261029'
R=N/'reports'


def read(p):return json.loads(Path(p).read_bytes())
def H(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def put(p,v):
 p=Path(p);p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);assert not p.exists(),str(p)
 p.write_bytes((json.dumps(v,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
def write(p,s):p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);p.write_bytes(s.encode('utf-8'))
def rep(s,a,b):assert s.count(a)==1,(a,s.count(a));return s.replace(a,b)
def assign(s,k,v):
 node=next(n for n in ast.parse(s).body if isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(x,ast.Name) and x.id==k for x in n.targets))
 lines=s.splitlines(True);return ''.join(lines[:node.lineno-1])+k+'='+pprint.pformat(v,width=120,sort_dicts=False)+'\n'+''.join(lines[node.end_lineno:])


def main():
 R.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
 seal=read(F/'FROZEN_SELECTED122_SOURCE_V3_INDEX.json')
 for row in seal['files']:assert H(row['path'])==row['SHA'],row['path']
 proof=read(F/'reports/SOURCE122_REWARD_PROCESS_OWN_FK_COUNTEREXAMPLES_CPU_V3_ACTUAL.json');assert proof['passed_source_CPU_only']

"""Build NEW123 typed payload from qualified116; binary hardlinks/metadata only."""
from pathlib import Path
import ast,copy,hashlib,json,os,pprint,runpy,shutil,time,traceback

L=T/'MOD4_H1_OVERNIGHT_FUDAN_SUPERMIMIC_REPAIR_GOAL_20261027'

R=N/'reports'


def read(p):return json.loads(Path(p).read_bytes())
def H(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def put(p,v):
 p=Path(p);p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);assert not p.exists(),str(p)
 p.write_bytes((json.dumps(v,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
def write(p,s):p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);p.write_bytes(s.encode('utf-8'))
def rep(s,a,b):assert s.count(a)==1,(a,s.count(a));return s.replace(a,b)
def assign(s,k,v):
 node=next(n for n in ast.parse(s).body if isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(x,ast.Name) and x.id==k for x in n.targets))
 lines=s.splitlines(True);return ''.join(lines[:node.lineno-1])+k+'='+pprint.pformat(v,width=120,sort_dicts=False)+'\n'+''.join(lines[node.end_lineno:])

def literal(p,name):

  stationary_height_bonus_exact_zero=True,actual_selected_sum_literal=True,
  actual_wheel_bottom_target_m=.27,process_goal_cues_in_policy_and_critic_max_abs=float(conditioned[...,43:49].abs().max()),
  process_cues_are_not_joint_velocity_targets=True,all_norm_buffers_literal_unchanged=True,

"""New necessary source CPU admission. No PT/raw/SDK/physics/network."""
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import ast

import hashlib
import json

import os
import sys

import traceback

T=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
