def put(name,value):
    p=R/name;assert not p.exists(),str(p)
    p.write_bytes((json.dumps(value,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())


def main():
    assert os.environ.get('CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES')==''
    out=R/'SOURCE122_REWARD_PROCESS_OWN_FK_COUNTEREXAMPLES_CPU_V3_ACTUAL.json'
    assert not out.exists()
    sys.path[:0]=[str(D/'source_v3'),str(B/'source')]
    import torch
    torch.set_num_threads(2)
    assert not torch.cuda.is_initialized()
    rng=torch.random.get_rng_state().clone()
    from jump_process_frame122_v2 import code,advance,unpack,with_code,inject_goal_cues,BASE
    from private_phase_frame_v1 import encode,decode,horizon_checks
    from direct_wheel_height_command_reward_v1 import CommandReward,modes,height_score
    from jump_process_command_reward122_v1 import evaluate,combine_closure_risk
    from flat91_mask_aware_goal_conditioning_v1 import masked_goal_conditioning
    from native_fudan_step_adapter_candidate_v2 import OwnFudanStepReference,OwnFudanStepMdp
    from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.geometry import unflatten_state
    p=CommandReward();checks=[]
    def check(value,label):
        assert bool(value),label
        checks.append(label)

    # All valid code values and boundary clocks, over all original250 delays.
    legal=[c for c in range(256) if ((c>>1)&7)<=4]
    ages=torch.tensor([0,9,10,179,180,199,200,1999,2000],dtype=torch.long)
    a=ages[None,:,None].expand(250,-1,len(legal));ids=torch.arange(250)[:,None,None].expand_as(a)
    meta=torch.tensor(legal)[None,None,:].expand_as(a)
    f=encode(a,ids,meta);r=decode(f)
    check(torch.equal(r['public_age'],a) and torch.equal(r['delay_ticks'],ids),'metadata cannot alias age or delay')
    check(r['valid_actual'].all(),'all declared boundary metadata frames decode')
    look=horizon_checks(f,32,kind='world')
    check(torch.equal(look['next_public_age'],a+32),'future32 age lookup retains metadata')
    check(not bool(decode(encode(torch.tensor([20]),torch.tensor([0]),torch.tensor([255])))['valid_actual'][0]),'aircount7 metadata rejected')

    ready=torch.tensor([True]);false=torch.tensor([False]);zero2=torch.zeros(1,2,dtype=torch.bool)
    c=code(ready=ready,air_controls=torch.tensor([0]),launched=false,crossed2=zero2,positive_vz=false)
    frame=with_code(torch.tensor([200]),c)
    for j in range(4):
        frame=advance(frame,clean_air=ready,root_vz=torch.tensor([.3]),
            previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.20,.20]]),next_front2=torch.tensor([[.21,.21]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.tensor([[.01,.01]]))
        check(bool(unpack(frame)['launched'][0])==(j==3),'four40ms planning air controls launch '+str(j))
    left=advance(frame,clean_air=ready,root_vz=torch.tensor([.2]),previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.34,.30]]),
        next_front2=torch.tensor([[.36,.32]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.tensor([[.28,.28]]))
    check(unpack(left)['crossed2'].tolist()==[[True,False]],'left can cross before right')
    both=advance(left,clean_air=ready,root_vz=torch.tensor([-.1]),previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.36,.34]]),
        next_front2=torch.tensor([[.38,.36]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.tensor([[.28,.28]]))
    check(unpack(both)['crossed'].all(),'asynchronous both-wheel crossing retained')
    landed=advance(both,clean_air=false,root_vz=torch.tensor([0.]),previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.6,.6]]),
        next_front2=torch.tensor([[.6,.6]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.tensor([[.27,.27]]))
    check(unpack(landed)['crossed'].all() and unpack(landed)['air_controls'].eq(0).all(),'landing retains path history')
    static=advance(with_code(torch.tensor([300]),c),clean_air=false,root_vz=torch.tensor([0.]),
        previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.6,.6]]),next_front2=torch.tensor([[.6,.6]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.tensor([[.27,.27]]))
    check(not unpack(static)['launched'].any() and not unpack(static)['crossed'].any(),'static top cannot invent jump/cross')

    raw_goal=torch.zeros(2,1460);raw_goal.reshape(2,10,146)[:,:,73:86]=1
    encoded=inject_goal_cues(raw_goal,torch.cat((torch.tensor([300]),landed)))
    normalized=torch.linspace(-30,30,2920).reshape(2,1460)
    conditioned=masked_goal_conditioning(normalized,encoded).reshape(2,10,146)
    check(torch.equal(encoded[0],raw_goal[0]),'zero process preserves original goal bytes')
    check(conditioned[...,43:49].abs().max()<=.500001,'cue bound independent of inherited normalization')
    check(torch.equal(conditioned[0,:,:73],normalized[0].reshape(10,146)[:,:73]*raw_goal[0].reshape(10,146)[:,73:]),'false cue conditioning literal')

    def scenario(z=0.,vx=0.,vz=0.,support=100.,x=.08,cross=False,launch=False,request=1.,closure=0.):
        low=torch.tensor([[[x-.04,-.2,z],[x-.04,.2,z]]],dtype=torch.float32)
        high=low+torch.tensor([.08,.04,.08])
        velocity=torch.tensor([[vx,0.,vz]])
        m=dict(wheel_bottom2=low[...,2],wheel_high2=high,wheel_bottom_velocity2=torch.full((1,2),vz),
            world_velocity3=velocity,world_angular_velocity3=torch.zeros(1,3),request_active=torch.tensor([request]),
            heading_cos=torch.ones(1),upright_cos=torch.ones(1),root_y=torch.zeros(1))
        mode=modes(low,high,torch.full((1,2),support),torch.tensor([x]),p)
        return m,mode,evaluate(m,mode,torch.tensor([.023]),torch.ones(1),launched=torch.tensor([launch]),crossed=torch.tensor([cross]),closure_m=torch.tensor([closure]),public_age=torch.tensor([300]),p=p)
    _,_,wrong=scenario(z=.27,x=.70)
    _,_,right=scenario(z=.27,x=.70,cross=True,launch=True)
    check((right['total']-wrong['total'])>.1,'same endpoint distinguishes valid jump/cross history')
    check(wrong['selected_terms']['supported_top_after_qualified_cross']==0,'standing-top no landing bonus')
    floor=[]
    for v in (0.,.25,.5):
        rewards=[]
        for x in torch.linspace(.056,.30,257):
            _,_,r=scenario(x=float(x),vx=v)
            rewards.append(float(r['total']))
        check(min(b-a for a,b in zip(rewards,rewards[1:]))>=-1e-7,'supported-floor approach monotonic v='+str(v))
        floor.append(dict(vx=v,first=rewards[0],last=rewards[-1]))
    _,_,forcefree=scenario(x=.20,vx=.5,support=0.)
    check(forcefree['selected_terms']['qualified_near_both_wheel_height270']==0 and forcefree['selected_terms']['before_edge_both_wheel_upward_command']==0,'force-zero floor alone no jump bonus')
    _,_,stationary=scenario(x=.20,z=.27,vx=0.,support=0.)
    check(stationary['selected_terms']['qualified_near_both_wheel_height270']==0,'stationary high hop no height bonus')
    _,_,rise=scenario(x=.20,z=.01,vx=.5,vz=2.08,support=0.)
    _,_,low=scenario(x=.20,z=.01,vx=.5,vz=0.,support=0.)
    check(rise['total']>low['total'],'real wheel ascent beats stationary1cm bounce')
    _,_,off=scenario(request=0.,closure=.003)
    check(torch.equal(off['total'],torch.tensor([.023])),'offrequest recovery literal')
    _,_,near_failure=scenario(vx=0.,closure=.003)
    positives=sum(value for name,value in near_failure['selected_terms'].items() if name not in ('closure_compliance_cost','original_nonrequest_recovery'))
    check(torch.equal(near_failure['total'],.5*positives),'saturated closure cost preserves half-positive continuation reward')
    # Even a moving landing must score above its unloaded same-position case.
    _,_,air=scenario(z=.27,x=.70,vx=.15,support=0.,launch=True,cross=True)
    _,_,top=scenario(z=.27,x=.70,vx=.15,support=1000.,launch=True,cross=True)
    check(top['total']>air['total'],'moving touchdown has no old reward valley')
    for z in (0.,.01,.1,.27,.4,.6):
        for v in (-2.,0.,.15,.5,2.):
            for support in (0.,1.,10.,1000.):
                _,_,r=scenario(z=z,x=.70,vx=v,support=support,launch=True,cross=True,closure=.001)
                check((r['total']>=-1e-7).all() and (r['total']<=.200001).all(),'finite request reward bound')
    predicted=torch.tensor([.0013072160072624683,.0017726129153743386,.0012448718771338463],requires_grad=True)
    risk=combine_closure_risk(torch.zeros(3),predicted,torch.full((3,),431))
    risk.sum().backward()
    check((risk>0).all() and torch.isfinite(predicted.grad).all() and (predicted.grad>0).all(),'all121 underestimated failures now receive nonzero smooth constraint risk')
    check(combine_closure_risk(torch.zeros(1),torch.tensor([.0023]),torch.tensor([431])).item()==1.,'original2mm certain stop retained')
    check(combine_closure_risk(torch.zeros(1),torch.tensor([.0023]),torch.tensor([9])).item()==0.,'original10control spawn grace retained')

    document=json.loads((B/'source/OWN_FUDAN_STEP_TERMINAL_GOAL_CANDIDATE_V2.json').read_bytes())
    provider=OwnFudanStepReference(document).bind_named_order(document['joint_names'],scope='source_CPU_fixture')
    mdp=OwnFudanStepMdp(provider,json.loads((B/'job0/assets/import_contract.json').read_bytes()),
        json.loads((B/'source/OWN_STEP_WHEEL_GEOMETRY_FROM_R2_SOURCE.json').read_bytes()),provider.metadata['actuator_leg_joint_names'])
    packed=torch.cat((provider.neutral,torch.zeros(54)))[None].expand(4,-1).clone()
    packed[:,7]=torch.tensor([0.,.25,.5,.5]);packed[:,0]=torch.tensor([.056,.10,.20,.60])
    packed[3,2]+=.27
    state=unflatten_state(packed)
    frames=encode(torch.tensor([199,200,300,431]),torch.arange(4))
    actions=torch.zeros(4,6)
    values=mdp.objective_terms(state,frames,actions,actions)
    check(torch.isfinite(values['total']).all(),'actual own30joint FK / full legacy API integration finite')
    check(torch.equal(sum(values['selected_optimization_terms'].values()),values['total']),'selected terms exact sum')
    check(torch.isfinite(mdp.soft_termination(state,frames)).all(),'actual ownFK closure risk finite')
    check(mdp.goal(frames,10).shape==(4,1460),'goal1460 unchanged')
    check(torch.equal(rng,torch.random.get_rng_state()),'RNG unchanged')
    check(not torch.cuda.is_initialized(),'no CUDA')
    result=dict(epoch=time.time(),passed_source_CPU_only=True,check_count=len(checks),checks=checks,
        endpoint_wrong_reward=float(wrong['total']),endpoint_valid_reward=float(right['total']),
        floor_approach=floor,lowhop_reward=float(low['total']),ascending_wheels_reward=float(rise['total']),
        moving_unloaded_reward=float(air['total']),moving_supported_reward=float(top['total']),
        source121_predicted_error_constraint_risk=risk.detach().tolist(),
        own_FK_actual_geometry_and_API_checked=True,request_reward_bounds=[0.,.20],
        process_flags_are_planning_surrogate_not_claimed_physical_flight_truth=True,
        no_checkpoint_raw_model_SDK_GPU_physics_remote_or_training=True,
        not_runtime_admission_or_actual270_success=True)

    sys.path[:0]=[str(D/'source_v2'),str(B/'source')]
    import torch
    torch.set_num_threads(2)

    from jump_process_frame122_v2 import code,advance,unpack,with_code,inject_goal_cues,BASE
    from private_phase_frame_v1 import encode,decode,horizon_checks
    from direct_wheel_height_command_reward_v1 import CommandReward,modes,height_score
    from jump_process_command_reward122_v1 import evaluate,combine_closure_risk
    from flat91_mask_aware_goal_conditioning_v1 import masked_goal_conditioning
    from native_fudan_step_adapter_candidate_v2 import OwnFudanStepReference,OwnFudanStepMdp
    from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.geometry import unflatten_state
    p=CommandReward();checks=[]
    def check(value,label):
        assert bool(value),label
        checks.append(label)

    # All valid code values and boundary clocks, over all original250 delays.
    legal=[c for c in range(256) if ((c>>1)&7)<=4]
    ages=torch.tensor([0,9,10,179,180,199,200,1999,2000],dtype=torch.long)
    a=ages[None,:,None].expand(250,-1,len(legal));ids=torch.arange(250)[:,None,None].expand_as(a)
    meta=torch.tensor(legal)[None,None,:].expand_as(a)
    f=encode(a,ids,meta);r=decode(f)
    check(torch.equal(r['public_age'],a) and torch.equal(r['delay_ticks'],ids),'metadata cannot alias age or delay')
    check(r['valid_actual'].all(),'all declared boundary metadata frames decode')
    look=horizon_checks(f,32,kind='world')
    check(torch.equal(look['next_public_age'],a+32),'future32 age lookup retains metadata')
    check(not bool(decode(encode(torch.tensor([20]),torch.tensor([0]),torch.tensor([255])))['valid_actual'][0]),'aircount7 metadata rejected')

    ready=torch.tensor([True]);false=torch.tensor([False]);zero2=torch.zeros(1,2,dtype=torch.bool)
    c=code(ready=ready,air_controls=torch.tensor([0]),launched=false,crossed2=zero2,positive_vz=false)
    frame=with_code(torch.tensor([200]),c)
    for j in range(4):
        frame=advance(frame,clean_air=ready,root_vz=torch.tensor([.3]),
            previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.20,.20]]),next_front2=torch.tensor([[.21,.21]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.tensor([[.01,.01]]))
        check(bool(unpack(frame)['launched'][0])==(j==3),'four40ms planning air controls launch '+str(j))
    left=advance(frame,clean_air=ready,root_vz=torch.tensor([.2]),previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.34,.30]]),
        next_front2=torch.tensor([[.36,.32]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.tensor([[.28,.28]]))
    check(unpack(left)['crossed2'].tolist()==[[True,False]],'left can cross before right')
    both=advance(left,clean_air=ready,root_vz=torch.tensor([-.1]),previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.36,.34]]),
        next_front2=torch.tensor([[.38,.36]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.tensor([[.28,.28]]))
    check(unpack(both)['crossed'].all(),'asynchronous both-wheel crossing retained')
    landed=advance(both,clean_air=false,root_vz=torch.tensor([0.]),previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.6,.6]]),
        next_front2=torch.tensor([[.6,.6]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.tensor([[.27,.27]]))
    check(unpack(landed)['crossed'].all() and unpack(landed)['air_controls'].eq(0).all(),'landing retains path history')
    static=advance(with_code(torch.tensor([300]),c),clean_air=false,root_vz=torch.tensor([0.]),
        previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.6,.6]]),next_front2=torch.tensor([[.6,.6]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.tensor([[.27,.27]]))
    check(not unpack(static)['launched'].any() and not unpack(static)['crossed'].any(),'static top cannot invent jump/cross')

    raw_goal=torch.zeros(2,1460);raw_goal.reshape(2,10,146)[:,:,73:86]=1
    encoded=inject_goal_cues(raw_goal,torch.cat((torch.tensor([300]),landed)))
    normalized=torch.linspace(-30,30,2920).reshape(2,1460)
    conditioned=masked_goal_conditioning(normalized,encoded).reshape(2,10,146)
    check(torch.equal(encoded[0],raw_goal[0]),'zero process preserves original goal bytes')
    check(conditioned[...,43:49].abs().max()<=.500001,'cue bound independent of inherited normalization')
    check(torch.equal(conditioned[0,:,:73],normalized[0].reshape(10,146)[:,:73]*raw_goal[0].reshape(10,146)[:,73:]),'false cue conditioning literal')

    def scenario(z=0.,vx=0.,vz=0.,support=100.,x=.08,cross=False,launch=False,request=1.,closure=0.):
        low=torch.tensor([[[x-.04,-.2,z],[x-.04,.2,z]]],dtype=torch.float32)
        high=low+torch.tensor([.08,.04,.08])
        velocity=torch.tensor([[vx,0.,vz]])
        m=dict(wheel_bottom2=low[...,2],wheel_high2=high,wheel_bottom_velocity2=torch.full((1,2),vz),
            world_velocity3=velocity,world_angular_velocity3=torch.zeros(1,3),request_active=torch.tensor([request]),
            heading_cos=torch.ones(1),upright_cos=torch.ones(1),root_y=torch.zeros(1))
        mode=modes(low,high,torch.full((1,2),support),torch.tensor([x]),p)
        return m,mode,evaluate(m,mode,torch.tensor([.023]),torch.ones(1),launched=torch.tensor([launch]),crossed=torch.tensor([cross]),closure_m=torch.tensor([closure]),public_age=torch.tensor([300]),p=p)
    _,_,wrong=scenario(z=.27,x=.70)
    _,_,right=scenario(z=.27,x=.70,cross=True,launch=True)
    check((right['total']-wrong['total'])>.1,'same endpoint distinguishes valid jump/cross history')
    check(wrong['selected_terms']['supported_top_after_qualified_cross']==0,'standing-top no landing bonus')
    floor=[]
    for v in (0.,.25,.5):
        rewards=[]
        for x in torch.linspace(.056,.30,257):
            _,_,r=scenario(x=float(x),vx=v)
            rewards.append(float(r['total']))
        check(min(b-a for a,b in zip(rewards,rewards[1:]))>=-1e-7,'supported-floor approach monotonic v='+str(v))
        floor.append(dict(vx=v,first=rewards[0],last=rewards[-1]))
    _,_,forcefree=scenario(x=.20,vx=.5,support=0.)
    check(forcefree['selected_terms']['qualified_near_both_wheel_height270']==0 and forcefree['selected_terms']['before_edge_both_wheel_upward_command']==0,'force-zero floor alone no jump bonus')
    _,_,stationary=scenario(x=.20,z=.27,vx=0.,support=0.)
    check(stationary['selected_terms']['qualified_near_both_wheel_height270']==0,'stationary high hop no height bonus')
    _,_,rise=scenario(x=.20,z=.01,vx=.5,vz=2.08,support=0.)
    _,_,low=scenario(x=.20,z=.01,vx=.5,vz=0.,support=0.)
    check(rise['total']>low['total'],'real wheel ascent beats stationary1cm bounce')
    _,_,off=scenario(request=0.,closure=.003)
    check(torch.equal(off['total'],torch.tensor([.023])),'offrequest recovery literal')
    # Even a moving landing must score above its unloaded same-position case.
    _,_,air=scenario(z=.27,x=.70,vx=.15,support=0.,launch=True,cross=True)
    _,_,top=scenario(z=.27,x=.70,vx=.15,support=1000.,launch=True,cross=True)
    check(top['total']>air['total'],'moving touchdown has no old reward valley')
    for z in (0.,.01,.1,.27,.4,.6):
        for v in (-2.,0.,.15,.5,2.):
            for support in (0.,1.,10.,1000.):
                _,_,r=scenario(z=z,x=.70,vx=v,support=support,launch=True,cross=True,closure=.001)
                check((r['total']>=-.020001).all() and (r['total']<=.200001).all(),'finite request reward bound')
    predicted=torch.tensor([.0013072160072624683,.0017726129153743386,.0012448718771338463],requires_grad=True)
    risk=combine_closure_risk(torch.zeros(3),predicted,torch.full((3,),431))
    risk.sum().backward()
    check((risk>0).all() and torch.isfinite(predicted.grad).all() and (predicted.grad>0).all(),'all121 underestimated failures now receive nonzero smooth constraint risk')
    check(combine_closure_risk(torch.zeros(1),torch.tensor([.0023]),torch.tensor([431])).item()==1.,'original2mm certain stop retained')
    check(combine_closure_risk(torch.zeros(1),torch.tensor([.0023]),torch.tensor([9])).item()==0.,'original10control spawn grace retained')

    document=json.loads((B/'source/OWN_FUDAN_STEP_TERMINAL_GOAL_CANDIDATE_V2.json').read_bytes())
    provider=OwnFudanStepReference(document).bind_named_order(document['joint_names'],scope='source_CPU_fixture')
    mdp=OwnFudanStepMdp(provider,json.loads((B/'job0/assets/import_contract.json').read_bytes()),
        json.loads((B/'source/OWN_STEP_WHEEL_GEOMETRY_FROM_R2_SOURCE.json').read_bytes()),provider.metadata['actuator_leg_joint_names'])
    packed=torch.cat((provider.neutral,torch.zeros(54)))[None].expand(4,-1).clone()
    packed[:,7]=torch.tensor([0.,.25,.5,.5]);packed[:,0]=torch.tensor([.056,.10,.20,.60])
    packed[3,2]+=.27
    state=unflatten_state(packed)

    values=mdp.objective_terms(state,frames,actions,actions)
    check(torch.isfinite(values['total']).all(),'actual own30joint FK / full legacy API integration finite')
    check(torch.equal(sum(values['selected_optimization_terms'].values()),values['total']),'selected terms exact sum')
    check(torch.isfinite(mdp.soft_termination(state,frames)).all(),'actual ownFK closure risk finite')
    check(mdp.goal(frames,10).shape==(4,1460),'goal1460 unchanged')
    check(torch.equal(rng,torch.random.get_rng_state()),'RNG unchanged')
    check(not torch.cuda.is_initialized(),'no CUDA')
    result=dict(epoch=time.time(),passed_source_CPU_only=True,check_count=len(checks),checks=checks,
        endpoint_wrong_reward=float(wrong['total']),endpoint_valid_reward=float(right['total']),
        floor_approach=floor,lowhop_reward=float(low['total']),ascending_wheels_reward=float(rise['total']),
        moving_unloaded_reward=float(air['total']),moving_supported_reward=float(top['total']),
        source121_predicted_error_constraint_risk=risk.detach().tolist(),
        own_FK_actual_geometry_and_API_checked=True,request_reward_bounds=[-.02,.20],
        process_flags_are_planning_surrogate_not_claimed_physical_flight_truth=True,
        no_checkpoint_raw_model_SDK_GPU_physics_remote_or_training=True,
        not_runtime_admission_or_actual270_success=True)
    put(out.name,result)
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in result.items() if k!='checks'}),flush=True)

def main():
    assert os.environ.get('CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES')==''
    sys.path[:0]=[str(D/'source_v2'),str(B/'source')]
    import torch
    torch.set_num_threads(2);rng=torch.random.get_rng_state().clone()

    from jump_process_runtime122_v1 import install,current_process_code
    from jump_process_frame122_v2 import code,unpack,with_code
    class Phase:
        def __init__(self,env):
            self.env=env;self.crossed=torch.zeros(2,2,dtype=torch.bool)
            self.previous_front=torch.zeros(2,2);self.target_body_peak=torch.zeros(2)
            self.goal=SimpleNamespace(attempt=torch.ones(2,dtype=torch.bool),was_ready=torch.ones(2,dtype=torch.bool),
                positive_vz=torch.ones(2,dtype=torch.bool),launched=torch.ones(2,dtype=torch.bool),success=torch.zeros(2,dtype=torch.bool))
            low=torch.tensor([[[.12,-.2,.01],[.12,.2,.01]]]).expand(2,-1,-1).clone()+env.scene.env_origins[:,None,:]
            high=low+torch.tensor([.08,.04,.08])
            self.last_measured=dict(step_wheel_min=low,step_wheel_max=high)
            self.original_calls=0
        def physical(self,**kw):
            self.original_calls+=1
            if 'new_cross' in kw:self.crossed|=kw['new_cross']
            self.last_original_return=dict(air=kw['air'],sentinel=object())
            return self.last_original_return
    env=SimpleNamespace(num_envs=2,device='cpu',scene=SimpleNamespace(env_origins=torch.tensor([[0.,0.,0.],[2.,0.,0.]])))
    env.h1_phase=Phase(env);p=env.h1_phase
    witness=install(env)
    for i in range(16):
        value=p.physical(air=torch.ones(2,dtype=torch.bool))
        assert value is p.last_original_return and p.original_calls==i+1
        assert bool(p._reward122_launched.all())==(i==15)
    p.physical(air=torch.ones(2,dtype=torch.bool),new_cross=torch.tensor([[True,False],[False,False]]))
    assert p._reward122_crossed2.tolist()==[[True,False],[False,False]]
    p.physical(air=torch.ones(2,dtype=torch.bool),new_cross=torch.tensor([[False,True],[False,False]]))

    reset=next(n for n in cls.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='reset')
    ns=dict(torch=torch);exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[reset],type_ignores=[]),'<original Step270 reset>','exec'),ns)
    other_before=current_process_code(env)[1].clone()
    ns['reset'](p,torch.tensor([0]))
    assert current_process_code(env)[0]==0 and torch.equal(current_process_code(env)[1],other_before)
    assert p.original_calls==18

    provider=OwnFudanStepReference(doc).bind_named_order(doc['joint_names'],scope='source_CPU_fixture')
    mdp=OwnFudanStepMdp(provider,json.loads((B/'job0/assets/import_contract.json').read_bytes()),
        json.loads((B/'source/OWN_STEP_WHEEL_GEOMETRY_FROM_R2_SOURCE.json').read_bytes()),provider.metadata['actuator_leg_joint_names'])
    raw=torch.cat((provider.neutral,torch.zeros(54)))[None]
    s=list(unflatten_state(raw));g=mdp.wheel_geometry(tuple(s))
    s[0]=s[0].clone();s[0][:,0]+=.34-g['high'][...,0].amax(-1)
    s[0][:,2]+=.28-g['low'][...,2].amin(-1)
    s[2]=torch.tensor([[.5,0.,.2]])
    initial=tuple(s)
    metadata=code(ready=torch.ones(1,dtype=torch.bool),air_controls=torch.full((1,),4),
        launched=torch.ones(1,dtype=torch.bool),crossed2=torch.zeros(1,2,dtype=torch.bool),positive_vz=torch.ones(1,dtype=torch.bool))
    frames=with_code(torch.tensor([300]),metadata)
    class WM:
        def step(self,state,action,context):
            pos=state[0]+state[0].new_tensor([.02,0.,0.])
            return (pos,*state[1:])
    fake=SimpleNamespace(pi=lambda history,goal:torch.zeros(1,6),wm=WM(),mdp=mdp,cfg=SimpleNamespace(goal_frames=10))
    learner_path=D/'source_v2/supermimic_wheel_leg_v3/learner.py'
    tree=ast.parse(learner_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    c=next(x for x in tree.body if isinstance(x,ast.ClassDef) and x.name=='SuperMimicLearner')
    fn=next(x for x in c.body if isinstance(x,ast.FunctionDef) and x.name=='_imag_step')
    ns=dict(torch=torch,State=tuple);exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[fn],type_ignores=[]),str(learner_path),'exec'),ns)
    newstate,action,hist,nextframe,nextgoal=ns['_imag_step'](fake,initial,torch.zeros(1,10,86),torch.zeros(1,6),frames,torch.zeros(1,31),torch.zeros(1,31),mdp.goal(frames,10))
    assert unpack(nextframe)['crossed2'].sum().item()==1 and not unpack(nextframe)['crossed'].any()
    assert unpack(nextframe)['public_age'].item()==301
    # Own neutral FK has a78.7mm left/right front offset. Preserve the first
    # crossing and continue the real source metadata path until the other crosses.
    for _ in range(4):
        newstate,action,hist,nextframe,nextgoal=ns['_imag_step'](fake,newstate,hist,action,nextframe,torch.zeros(1,31),torch.zeros(1,31),nextgoal)
    assert unpack(nextframe)['crossed'].all() and unpack(nextframe)['public_age'].item()==305
    assert nextgoal.reshape(1,10,146)[...,120:122].eq(1).all()
    assert hist.shape==(1,10,86) and nextgoal.shape==(1,1460)
    assert torch.isfinite(mdp.reward(newstate,nextframe,action,torch.zeros_like(action))).all()
    # Exact original learned algorithm remains, with only the metadata transition call.
    old=(B/'source/supermimic_wheel_leg_v3/learner.py').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    new=learner_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    assert new.replace('        next_frames = self.mdp.advance_reward_frame(state, next_state, frames)',
                       '        next_frames = frames + 1')==old
    # New velocity fields agree with the derivative for a pure world translation.
    g=mdp.wheel_geometry(initial);speed=wheel_bottom_velocity2(g,mdp.wheel_geometry)
    translated=list(initial);translated[0]=initial[0]+initial[2]*.001
    derivative=(mdp.wheel_geometry(tuple(translated))['low'][...,2]-g['low'][...,2])/.001
    assert (speed-derivative).abs().max()<.001
    # Source122 risk and selected reward remain differentiable through own FK.
    q=initial[4].clone().requires_grad_(True)
    differentiable=(*initial[:4],q,*initial[5:])
    objective=mdp.reward(differentiable,frames,torch.zeros(1,6),torch.zeros(1,6)).sum()
    grad=torch.autograd.grad(objective,q)[0]
    assert torch.isfinite(grad).all()
    assert torch.equal(rng,torch.random.get_rng_state()) and not torch.cuda.is_initialized()
    result=dict(epoch=time.time(),passed_source_CPU_only=True,
        original400Hz_callback_invoked_exactly_once_per_call=18,original_return_identity_preserved=True,
        original_reset_zeros_new_metadata_and_preserves_other_env=True,
        original_success_counters_not_modified=True,duplicate_observer_rejected=True,
        actual_source_learner_imagination_step_propagates_cross_and_goal_cues=True,
        original_learner_text_inverse_exact_except_task_metadata_transition=True,
        wheel_bottom_point_velocity_translation_derivative_max_error=float((speed-derivative).abs().max()),
        own_FK_reward_q_gradient_finite=True,source_horizon32_goal10_dimensions_unchanged=True,
        no_saved_model_or_raw_read_or_actual_SDK_GPU_physics_or_training=True,
        synthetic_kinematic_fixture_not_a_physical_jump=True,not_runtime_admitted=True)

            pos=state[0]+state[0].new_tensor([.02,0.,0.])

  def __getattr__(self,name):
   obj=getattr(torch,name)
   if name not in ('full','zeros','tensor'):return obj
   def call(*args,**kwargs):
